# Sunday Clays — Plan 08: Core Pages Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or
> superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
> **Every implementer and reviewer runs on Opus** (master Global Constraints). Each task is one branch/worktree/PR
> (`task/08-<master id>-<slug>`), shipped per master §0 step 5 (stacked PRs, `ci-ok` green before merge).

**Goal:** Ship the member-facing core pages (events calendar + event detail, shooter directory + profile with charts,
club dashboard, home) and the three admin pages (imports with preview/commit/rollback, identity & rules, data issues &
ops) on top of the Plan 06 read APIs, the Plan 04 admin API and the Plan 07 frontend foundation.

**Architecture:** Each page lives in its own `frontend/src/features/<name>/` folder discovered by the Plan 01 registry
glob (C10): `routes.tsx` (lazy routes + nav), `api.ts` (TanStack Query hooks over the openapi-fetch `api` client, types
derived from the generated `paths`), `pages/`, `components/`, pure chart adapters in `charts.ts` that turn API rows into
`TabularData` + an `EChartsOption`, and `mocks.ts` (MSW fixture data + default handlers). Every data chart renders inside
Plan 07's `ChartFrame`/`ChartCard`. The events, shooters and home features host the three extension globs
(`eventSection.tsx`, `profileSection.tsx`, `homeWidget.tsx`) that Plans 10 and 11 plug into without editing these folders.

**Tech Stack:** React 19 · TypeScript (strict, `verbatimModuleSyntax`) · React Router 7 (`lazy` routes) · TanStack Query 5 ·
openapi-fetch + openapi-typescript · Apache ECharts (through Plan 07 builders/`ChartFrame`) · Tailwind v4 theme tokens ·
lucide-react · Vitest + Testing Library + user-event + MSW 2 + vitest-canvas-mock · Playwright (Chromium, 390×844 and
1440×900).

**Spec:** `/Users/bryanmoran/code/sunday-clays/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-27-sunday-clays-design.md`

**Master:** `/Users/bryanmoran/code/sunday-clays/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-00-master.md` (Architecture Contract
C1–C12 is binding; this plan implements master "Plan 08" exactly).

## Global Constraints
- Python `3.13`; Node `22` LTS; Postgres `17`; package managers `uv` (backend) and `pnpm` (frontend); lockfiles committed.
- Dependency ownership: Plan 01 T1 (backend) and T2 (frontend) declare the complete Phase-1 dependency set; later tasks never change `[project].dependencies`/`[dependency-groups]` in `backend/pyproject.toml`, `dependencies`/`devDependencies` in `frontend/package.json`, `uv.lock` or `pnpm-lock.yaml` (tool-config sections and scripts may change); a truly new package first lands in a controller `chore(deps): add <pkg>` PR (branch `chore/deps-<pkg>`, manifest + lockfile only) that the task rebases onto; lockfile rebase conflicts are never hand-merged — backend `git checkout origin/main -- backend/uv.lock && (cd backend && uv lock)`, frontend `pnpm install` — then commit (documented in CONTRIBUTING.md, Plan 01 T5); `tools/trophy_art` has its own `pyproject.toml` and is exempt.
- Coverage floor **90% lines AND 90% branches**, backend and frontend measured separately; ratchet may only rise.
- Lint/type: `ruff check` + `ruff format --check` + `mypy --strict` (backend); `eslint` (typescript-eslint strict) + `tsc --noEmit` + `prettier --check` (frontend); warnings held at 0 (`eslint --max-warnings 0`; ruff has no warning level).
- TDD: every production change starts from a failing test (superpowers:test-driven-development).
- Every round is 50 targets; `score` is an int in `0..50`.
- Round type values exactly: `sporting`, `super_sporting`, `unknown`. Rule: any station that day with an odd `target_count` ⇒ `super_sporting`; station data with all even ⇒ `sporting`; no station data ⇒ `unknown`; admin override wins.
- Shooter status values exactly: `member`, `guest`, `deceased`.
- Event dates are SQL `date` (no tz). Weather rows are keyed by local `America/Los_Angeles` wall-clock timestamps. The event window is 10:00–12:00 local and uses the hourly stamps 10:00, 11:00 and 12:00; C7 gives the per-field rules. Stored timestamps are `timestamptz` UTC.
- Units: °F, mph and inches, stored and displayed. Pressure is the one exception: it is stored in hPa (`pressure_hpa`) and displayed in inHg (hPa × 0.02953, 2 dp) via `lib/format.ts:formatPressure` (Plan 07 T4).
- Theme (ClaySmasher defaultBrand): surface `#1A4D2E`, elevated `#2D5F3F`, primary `#9E5530`, primary-container `#C76D3F`, accent `#E8A77A`, text `#FEFBF6`, text-muted `#D4E7DD`, outline `#D1E7DA`, outline-variant `#4A7C59`, error `#FEE2E2`, error-container `#DC2626`; font Roboto; radii card 12px, button 20px, sheet 16px. Dark-only UI.
- Viewports: mobile `390×844` and desktop `1440×900` are both first-class; every page has an e2e check at both.
- Host `sundayclays.claysmasher.com`; compose services exactly `caddy`, `api`, `worker`, `db`, `backup`; configuration only via env vars and `*_FILE` Docker secrets; no host bind mounts in `compose.yaml`. In the Swarm, TLS terminates at the existing Traefik (`traefik_public` external network, labels `traefik.http.routers.sundayclays.rule=Host(\`sundayclays.claysmasher.com\`)`, `entrypoints=websecure`, `tls.certresolver=cloudflare`); Caddy listens on plain `:80` inside the stack.
- Trophy art comes from imagen at `https://imagen.thehalf.io` via the dev-only tool `tools/trophy_art/` (credentials from env `IMAGEN_URL`, `IMAGEN_USERNAME`, `IMAGEN_PASSWORD`, never committed); the running app and CI never call imagen.
- Auth: exactly two roles, `viewer` and `admin` (admin ⊇ viewer); passwords supplied as argon2 hashes via secrets.
- Do NOT add a Claude code-review GitHub workflow.
- Branch per task `task/<NN>-<T>-<slug>` (NN = sub-plan, T = task); controller-only branches `chore/ratchet-wave-<N>` (the only branches that may change `ratchets/`); Conventional Commits; commit trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Agents: every implementer, reviewer, fix-loop escalation, plan author, critic and verifier runs on Opus — never pass a cheaper `model` to Agent or workflow `agent()`, whatever a skill's model-selection guidance says.
- PR topology: each dependency chain is one GitHub stacked PR stack (`gh stack`, extension `github/gh-stack`, installed 2026-09-27 on gh 2.101.0); independent chains are separate stacks off `main`; `ci.yml`'s `pull_request` trigger has no `branches:` filter so mid-stack PRs (whose base is another stack branch) run full CI.
- Migrations must be expand/contract compatible with the previous release's code (`api` runs `alembic upgrade head` on start, before uvicorn).
- Test fixtures are the real workbooks at `backend/tests/fixtures/scores_2026-09-27.xlsx` and `backend/tests/fixtures/stations_2026-09-27.xlsx` (copies of the originals; originals stay at repo root until the user deletes them).
- Wave 3 carries for UI tasks: every data chart renders in `ChartFrame`/`ChartCard` (Plan 07 T6), and each page's chart test uses `expectChartControls` from `src/test/charts.ts`; in-app links use `useRoundTypeLink`/`useRoundTypeHref` (`lib/roundTypes.ts`). Fixture facts: 7,480 rounds, 332 shooters, 360 events (310 held); gauge Sub-Gauge 9, 28 Gauge 8. Known gap, parked at the Wave 3 final review: the fullscreen view does not show ChartCard's chips or the truncation note.

## Review Focus
1. **Stale or partial scores file uploaded** (older copy missing recent weeks) → preview must list removed events/rows prominently and `commit` must refuse unless `confirm_removals=true`. Owner: Plan 03 Task 6 (test `test_commit_refuses_unconfirmed_removals`) and Plan 08 Task 5a (Vitest `commit stays disabled until removals are confirmed`).
2. **A remembered "That's me" id that no longer exists** (the shooter was merged into another, so
   `GET /api/shooters/{id}` → 404) → the home "me" panel says the profile could not be found and offers "Choose again",
   which clears `lib/me`; never a blank panel or an endless retry. Owner: Task 5 (Plan 08 T4), Vitest
   `stale me id shows choose-again and clears it`.
3. **Events without a complete scored field** — attendance-only dates (43 before 2020, 6 later), a station-only date
   with no head count, and the single `incomplete_results` date 2024-11-10 (7 of 27) → the event page says
   "Attendance only — N shooters, no scores recorded", "No scores recorded for this event", or "Partial results — 7 of 27
   shooters recorded."; never `NaN`, `null` or an empty results table. Owner: Task 1 (Plan 08 T1), Vitest
   `attendance-only event shows the head count empty state`, `station-only date says no scores recorded`,
   `incomplete event shows the partial results note`; e2e `attendance-only event shows the head count`.
4. **Ties and multi-round days in event results** (2026-09-27: Stockton and Finnegan both 49; 2026-08-30: Nickerson 40/40)
   → tied winners both show `T1`, a shooter's extra round shows rank `—` and an `R2` badge, and the rating change appears
   only on the best round. Owner: Task 1, Vitest `tied best rounds share a T-rank`,
   `extra round shows no rank and an R2 badge`; e2e `tied winners share first place`.
5. **Admin action rejected by the server** (400 `unreadable_file` for a non-workbook, 409 `not_pending` when the import
   was already committed from another tab, 403 when the session is no longer an admin one) → the server's
   `error.message` (403: "Admins only — sign in with the admin password.") appears next to the control that failed and
   the page stays usable (a viewer who opens an admin URL first gets Plan 07's `RequireRole` "Admins only" page,
   Decision D13). Owner: Task 6
   (Plan 08 T5a), Vitest `upload of an unreadable file shows the server message`, `commit conflict shows the server
   message`, `a viewer session sees the admins-only message`.

## Decisions
Choices made here where the master is silent. Cross-plan auditors: D3, D5, D6 and D22 are the names other plans
consume or provide.

- **D1 Task map.** Task 1 = T1 events (`task/08-1-events`), Task 2 = T2a shooters (`task/08-2a-shooters`), Task 3 =
  T2b profile charts (`task/08-2b-profile-charts`), Task 4 = T3 club (`task/08-3-club`), Task 5 = T4 home
  (`task/08-4-home`), Task 6 = T5a admin imports (`task/08-5a-admin-imports`), Task 7 = T5b admin identity
  (`task/08-5b-admin-identity`), Task 8 = T5c admin ops (`task/08-5c-admin-ops`).
- **D2 No imports between concurrent siblings.** T1, T2a, T3, T4 and T5a run concurrently, so none imports another's
  files. Each feature's `api.ts` declares hooks for every endpoint it reads, with `queryKey = [<path template>, …params]`
  so identical requests share the TanStack cache at runtime. Only dependents import their parent: Task 3 imports Task 2
  files; Tasks 7–8 import from Task 6 `features/admin/api.ts` (`JsonOf`, `ShooterOption`), `features/admin/format.ts`
  (`formatTimestamp`, `formatDay`), `features/admin/mocks.ts` (`shooterMatches`, `doneJob`) and
  `features/admin/components/{JobProgress,ShooterPicker,AdminError}.tsx` (which call `useJob`/`useShooterSearch`). For the same reason tiny display
  helpers (`formatDay`, `formatSigned`) exist per feature in `features/<name>/format.ts`; folding them into
  `lib/format.ts` is a later refactor, not part of this plan.
- **D3 Response types come from `paths`, never from schema model names.** Every `api.ts` defines
  `type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } } ? R : never` and
  derives e.g. `EventDetail = JsonOf<paths['/api/events/{date}']['get']>`, so only URL paths (C8) and field names bind
  Plan 08 to Plans 03/04/06. The fields each task reads are listed under its **Consumes**, copied from the response
  models exactly as Plan 06 T1/T5–T9, Plan 04 T2–T3 and Plan 03 T3/T7 declare them (`MetaOut`, `EventSummaryOut`,
  `EventDetailOut`, `ShooterSummaryOut`, `ShooterDetailOut`, `ShooterRoundOut`, `RatingOut`, `SplitOut`,
  `ShooterInsightsOut`, `ClubSummaryOut`, `AttendanceOut`, `CohortOut`, `DistributionOut`, `RegularsOut`,
  `ConversionYearOut`, `ParityYearOut`, `ClubTrendsOut`, `DuplicateSideOut`, `DataIssueOut`, `AuditEntryOut`,
  `RuleOut`, `RuleMutationOut`, `JobOut`; `ImportPreview`, `ScoresDiff`, `StationsDiff`, `FindingOut`,
  `ImportSummary` exactly as C5). Every mock typed with one of these carries every required field.
  - **Path templates are the C8 literals**: `/api/events/{date}` and `/api/shooters/{id}` (+ `/rounds`, `/rating`,
    `/splits`, `/insights`), called with `params: { path: { date } }` / `params: { path: { id } }` — the same
    convention Plans 04, 10 and 11 already follow with `Path(alias=…)`.
  - **Cross-plan requirement (blocking; for the cross-plan critic, since this plan may not edit Plan 06).** Plan 06 as
    written declares `@router.get("/api/events/{event_date}")` (T5) and `/api/shooters/{shooter_id}…` (T6, T8), which
    would generate the path keys `{event_date}`/`{shooter_id}` instead of C8's. C8's route strings are binding, so this
    plan keeps `{date}`/`{id}` and Plan 06 T5/T6/T8 must bind those arguments as
    `event_date: Annotated[date, Path(alias="date")]` and `shooter_id: Annotated[int, Path(alias="id")]` with the C8
    route strings (the fix a review of this plan also preferred over renaming paths here). The second requirement
    raised earlier is already met as written: Plan 06 T5's `EventResultOut` declares `name_key: str` (the
    `rounds.name_key` identity key, C4), which Task 7 needs to build the C5 `score_override`/`hide_round` payload
    `{event_date, name_key, ordinal}`, and Plan 04 T3's `DuplicateSideOut` declares `name_key: str`. Tasks 1, 5 and 7
    type `EventResult` with a required `name_key: string` and their mocks set it; Task 7's mocks set
    `DuplicateSideOut.name_key`.
  - Each task's Step 0 regenerates the schema and checks these keys; if they are missing, the implementer stops and
    reports BLOCKED (cross-plan defect) and never renames a path or drops a field locally.
- **D4 MSW handler ownership by path prefix.** `events/mocks.ts`: `/api/events*`; `shooters/mocks.ts`:
  `/api/shooters*` (viewer); `club/mocks.ts`: `/api/club/*`; `home/mocks.ts`: `/api/meta`; `admin/mocks.ts`:
  `/api/admin/imports*`, `/api/admin/jobs/*`; `admin-identity/mocks.ts`: `/api/admin/possible-duplicates`,
  `/api/admin/rules*`, `/api/admin/shooters/merge`, `/api/admin/shooters/:id/rename`, `/api/admin/shooters/:id/status`;
  `admin-ops/mocks.ts`: `/api/admin/data-issues`, `/api/admin/audit`, `/api/admin/recompute`,
  `/api/admin/shooters/:id/aliases`. Handlers use `*/api/...` patterns (any origin) and are branch-free (a fixed
  response, at most parameterised by a path param), because `mocks.ts` counts toward coverage. Each feature's
  `routes.test.tsx` deliberately runs on its own default handlers, so every handler line executes; every other test pins
  its inputs with `server.use(...)`, and any test that touches an endpoint owned by another feature pins it, so another
  feature's default handler can never change what a test sees.
- **D5 Extension-glob types (Produces for Plans 10 and 11).**
  `features/events/sections.ts`: `export type EventSection = { id: string; title: string; order: number; Component: FC<{ date: string }> }`.
  `features/shooters/sections.ts`: `export type ProfileSection = { id: string; title: string; order: number; Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }> }`.
  `features/home/widgets.ts`: `export type HomeWidget = { id: string; order: number; slot: 'main' | 'me'; Component: FC<{ meId: number | null }> }`.
  Hosts sort by `order`, then `id`, and wrap each event/profile section in a `Card` titled `title` (a section renders
  its own body only; home widgets render themselves with no wrapper). Page components take an optional
  `sections`/`widgets` prop whose default is the glob result, so page tests pin it to `[]` and later plugins never change
  a Plan 08 test.
- **D6 Home widget slots.** `main` widgets always render in the main column with `meId` (possibly `null`); `me` widgets
  render inside the me panel, below the odometer, only when a me id is set and its profile loaded.
- **D7 Season calendar = navigation grid.** It is a grid of 44 px date links (Sundays of the year plus any non-Sunday
  event date such as 2019-01-14), not a data chart, so it is not wrapped in `ChartFrame`.
- **D8 Result ranks.** Rank = the best round's `event_rank`; `T<n>` when ≥2 best rounds share it; non-best rounds show
  `—` and an `R<ordinal>` badge when `ordinal > 1`; rating Δ = the result's `rating_delta` (Plan 06 T5:
  `mu_after − mu_before`, set on every round of the shooter) shown on the best round only. Signed numbers use U+2212
  for minus and round before signing (−0.04 → `0.0`).
- **D9 Odometer and insights.** Hit rate is computed as `clays_broken / clays_thrown` (so the unit of
  `odometer.hit_pct` never matters); `favorite_month` is read as 1–12; `bad_day_rate` and `avg_percentile` are fractions
  0–1; the form label is Hot at `form ≥ +3`, Cold at `form ≤ −3`, else Steady (Plan 06 T8 thresholds).
- **D10 PB markers** on the score trend follow C12 `personal_bests`: the day's best round is a PB when it is greater
  than every round on earlier dates and the shooter has ≥5 rounds on earlier dates; at most one marker per day.
- **D11 Club comparisons.** "Distribution vs club" compares the shooter's score shares with the club's all-years
  shares (sum of the `GET /api/club/distribution?by=year` group counts). Rolling-8 lines on the club page are the
  server's `top_score_rolling8`, `median_rolling8` and `difficulty_rolling8` (Plan 06 T9, `rolling(8, min_periods=1)`
  over held events), rounded to one decimal; there is no client-side rolling mean.
- **D12 Chart building.** Plain charts call the Plan 07 builders (assumed signatures in D22). The rating curve (band +
  peak) and the dual-axis score/adjusted trend with PB markers need series the generic builders do not offer, so
  `features/shooters/charts.ts` composes those two `EChartsOption`s itself (pure, unit-tested, no tooltip `formatter`
  functions, so no data-derived HTML is ever built).
- **D13 Admin routing.** Routes `/admin` (imports), `/admin/imports/:id`, `/admin/identity`, `/admin/ops`; nav items
  Imports (`Upload`, order 900), Identity (`UserCog`, 910), Data & ops (`Wrench`, 920), all `adminOnly: true`. Every
  admin route element is wrapped in Plan 07's `<RequireRole role="admin">` (Plan 07 D10, the producer's rule for Plan
  08's admin pages): a viewer session gets its "Admins only" page and no admin request is sent. Any admin call that
  still returns 403 (e.g. the admin session was replaced by a viewer login in another tab) renders "Admins only — sign
  in with the admin password." through `AdminError`. Each admin `routes.test.tsx` renders through Plan 07's
  `renderRoutes(routes, { route, role: 'admin' })` and has one viewer case.
- **D14 Import preview rules.** Duplicate (`duplicate_of !== null`): Plan 03 (its Decision 7) stages nothing and returns
  the existing pending or committed import's stored preview with `import_id === duplicate_of`, so the preview page *is*
  that import's page and its actions follow that import's status. A committed (or not yet known) status shows "Already
  imported as import #N. Nothing to commit." and no actions; a still-pending one shows "Already uploaded as import #N,
  which is still pending: review it below, then commit or discard it." and keeps Commit and Discard. (A later
  `GET /api/admin/imports/{id}` returns the same preview with `duplicate_of: null`.) Discard is offered for every
  `pending` import. Commit is disabled while removals are unconfirmed, while the status is not `pending`, and after a
  successful commit; ERROR findings never disable it. After a commit from the page the panel and its job progress stay
  on screen even once the refreshed list reports `committed` (so "Rebuilding live data: Done" never vanishes). Findings
  are shown in three sections: "Will not be
  imported" (severity `error`), "Station vs score" (`station_score_mismatch`, `station_name_unmatched`,
  `station_round_ambiguous` that are not errors), "Other findings" (grouped by code, warnings before info). The import's
  status comes from `GET /api/admin/imports` (C5 `ImportPreview` has no status).
- **D15 Jobs.** `useJob(id)` polls `GET /api/admin/jobs/{id}` every 1000 ms until `done` or `failed`; `JobProgress`
  calls `onDone` once per job id. Rollback needs a second inline click ("Confirm roll back"); no `window.confirm`.
- **D16 e2e mutation fixture.** `frontend/e2e/fixtures/stations_variant.xlsx` = the stations fixture loaded with
  `data_only=True`, tab `9 13 26` deleted, `9 6 26!B31` (Marsden, Wylie, station 4) changed 4 → 5 (station total 31 vs
  score 30), saved with openpyxl (recipe in Task 6). Its preview shows exactly one "Station vs score" finding (Marsden,
  Wylie) and `2026-09-06` under replaced events. Re-running `admin-mutations` locally needs a fresh stack
  (`docker compose … down -v`), because imports are immutable and the second upload is a duplicate.
- **D17 Playwright project.** Task 6 adds project `admin-mutations` (`testMatch: /admin-mutations\.spec\.ts/`,
  `dependencies: ['mobile', 'desktop']`, `workers: 1` — `TestProject.workers` exists since Playwright 1.52 —, desktop
  Chrome at 1440×900, `storageState: ADMIN_STATE`) and `testIgnore: /admin-mutations\.spec\.ts/` on the `mobile` and
  `desktop` projects. Read-only admin specs use `test.use({ storageState: ADMIN_STATE })` (Plan 04 D19), with
  `ADMIN_STATE` imported from Plan 04 T1's `e2e/authState.ts` (in `playwright.config.ts` next to `VIEWER_STATE`), never
  the literal path.
- **D18 Home me panel.** Last result = the best round on the shooter's latest attended date: score, finish (the
  Quigley ordinal of `event_rank`, e.g. `7th`; `—` when null) and rating move (`mu_after − mu_before`). "Rank moves" in
  the master task line is rendered as that rating move plus the finish (leaderboard positions belong to Plan 09, which
  can add a `me`-slot widget).
- **D19 Directory.** Lists every shooter by default (inactive and deceased included; the memorial marker shows in the
  list too); the "Active only" toggle sends `active=true`. Search text and the toggle live in the URL (`q`, `active`).
- **D20 Club turnout card.** `ChartCard` with spec
  `querySpec({ metric: 'attendance', agg: 'avg', group_by: ['condition'] })` (Plan 07 D18: `querySpec` fills the
  server-defaulted fields the generated `QuerySpec` type marks required),
  `allowedGroupBy: ['condition', 'temp_band', 'wind_band', 'precip_band']`, `allowedMetrics: ['attendance']`,
  `chartTypes: ['bar']`. Its e2e asserts only the title: with `WEATHER_ENABLED=false` the Explorer returns one
  unspecified-condition group rather than an empty state. The weather card on the event page is the Plan 08 weather
  surface whose empty state e2e asserts.
- **D21 Test layout and commands.** Tests sit next to the code as `*.test.ts(x)`; focused runs use
  `pnpm exec vitest run <file>`; the full frontend gate is `pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm exec prettier --check . && pnpm exec vitest run --coverage`.
  Code blocks in this plan are not guaranteed prettier-exact: every task runs `pnpm exec prettier --write` on its files
  before the gate.
- **D22 Consumed Plan 07 names — verbatim from Plan 07's task code** (Plan 07 T1–T6 and its Decisions D9, D12, D13,
  D16–D18). Each task copies the entries it uses into its own **Consumes** and its Step 0 checks they exist. If a
  merged export is missing or its signature differs, the implementer stops and reports NEEDS_CONTEXT with the actual
  export; it never edits Plan 07's files and never guesses a mapping.
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, options?: RenderOptions): ProvidersResult` with
    `interface RenderOptions { route?: string; path?: string; queryClient?: QueryClient; role?: Role | null | 'unset' }`
    (`route` = initial URL, default `'/'`; `ui` is mounted at `path`, default `'*'`, so pages that read `useParams`
    are wrapped in their own `<Routes><Route path=…/></Routes>`; `role` seeds the session, default `'viewer'`) and
    `interface ProvidersResult extends RenderResult { user: UserEvent; queryClient: QueryClient; router: ReturnType<typeof createMemoryRouter> }`;
    queries never retry. Also `renderRoutes(routes: RouteObject[], options: RenderOptions = {}): ProvidersResult` (a
    route tree in a memory router inside the production providers, session seeded by `role`; used by the admin route
    tests).
  - `src/features/auth/components/RequireRole.tsx` (Plan 07 T3):
    `RequireRole({ role = 'viewer', children }: { role?: Role; children: ReactNode })` with `type Role = 'viewer' | 'admin'`
    — no session → `/login?next=…`; a viewer under `role="admin"` gets `EmptyState` titled "Admins only" ("Log out and
    log in with the admin password to open this page."). Plan 07 D10: admin pages wrap their elements in it (D13).
  - `src/test/msw/server.ts`: `server` (MSW `setupServer`, `onUnhandledRequest: 'error'`); `src/test/msw/handlers.ts`
    merges every `features/*/mocks.ts` `handlers` export.
  - `src/api/client.ts`: `api` (`createClient<paths>`, openapi-fetch) and
    `unwrap<T>(request: Promise<FetchResult<T>>): Promise<T>` with
    `interface FetchResult<T> { data?: T; error?: unknown; response: Response }` (any non-2xx throws `ApiError`);
    `src/api/errors.ts`: `class ApiError extends Error { readonly status: number; readonly code: string }` whose
    `message` is the server's `error.message`.
  - `src/lib/useUrlState.ts`: `interface Codec<T> { parse: (raw: string) => T | null; serialize: (value: T) => string }`;
    `useUrlState<T>(key: string, codec: Codec<T>, defaultValue: T): [T, (next: T) => void]` (missing or invalid →
    `defaultValue`; writing the default deletes the key; history `replace`); ready codecs
    `stringCodec: Codec<string>`, `intCodec: Codec<number>` (`/^-?\d+$/`), `boolCodec: Codec<boolean>` (`1`/`0`),
    `enumCodec<T extends string>(values: readonly T[]): Codec<T>`. No task hand-rolls a codec.
  - `src/lib/roundTypes.ts`: `ROUND_TYPES`, `type RoundTypeValue = 'sporting' | 'super_sporting' | 'unknown'`,
    `useRoundTypes(): [RoundTypeValue[], (next: RoundTypeValue[]) => void]` (the global filter in the `rt` query
    parameter; `[]` = no filter). Hooks call `const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();`, put `roundTypes` in the query key
    and send it as `round_type` (C10). `useRoundTypeLink(path: string): To` (Plan 07 T2) keeps `?rt=` on a link: every
    in-app link (to events, shooters, …) uses it rather than a bare path string, so the filter survives navigation
    (C10); it is a hook, so list rows use a small link component that calls it.
  - `src/lib/format.ts`: `formatPressure(hPa: number | null | undefined): string` (hPa × 0.02953, 2 dp, `' inHg'`
    suffix; `—` for null). `src/lib/me.ts`: `getMe(): number | null`, `setMe(id: number): void`, `clearMe(): void`.
  - `src/app/registry.ts`: `interface NavItem { label: string; path: string; icon: LucideIcon; order: number; mobileTab?: boolean; adminOnly?: boolean }`.
  - `src/components/ui/` (one named export per file):
    `Card({ title, subtitle, actions, className, children }: { title?: ReactNode; subtitle?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; className?: string; children?: ReactNode })`
    (a `<section>` labelled by its title, i.e. role `region`);
    `Stat({ label, value, delta, formatDelta, hint }: { label: string; value: ReactNode; delta?: number | null; formatDelta?: (magnitude: number) => string; hint?: string })`;
    `EmptyState({ title, description, action, icon }: { title: string; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode })`;
    `Skeleton({ label = 'Loading', lines = 3, className }: { label?: string; lines?: number; className?: string })` (role `status`);
    `Button(props: ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: ButtonVariant; loading?: boolean; icon?: ReactNode })`
    with `type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'tonal' | 'ghost' | 'danger'` (default `'primary'`, default `type="button"`);
    `Chip({ children, selected, onClick, onRemove, removeLabel, icon }: { children: ReactNode; selected?: boolean; onClick?: () => void; onRemove?: () => void; removeLabel?: string; icon?: ReactNode })`
    (with `onClick` it renders a `<button aria-pressed>`);
    `Toggle({ label, checked, onChange, disabled }: { label: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (checked: boolean) => void; disabled?: boolean })`
    (a `role="switch"` button named by `label`).
  - `src/components/charts/types.ts`: `type ColumnType = 'date' | 'number' | 'string' | 'int'`,
    `interface TabularColumn { key: string; label: string; type: ColumnType }`, `type Cell = string | number | null`,
    `type TabularRow = Record<string, Cell>`, `interface TabularData { columns: TabularColumn[]; rows: TabularRow[] }`,
    `type ChartType = 'bar' | 'line' | 'heatmap'`.
  - `src/components/charts/ChartFrame.tsx`: `ChartFrame(props: ChartFrameProps)` with
    `interface ChartFrameProps { title: string; subtitle?: string | undefined; option: EChartsOption; columns: TabularData['columns']; rows: TabularData['rows']; csvName: string; ariaLabel: string; urlKey: string; zoom?: ZoomMode | undefined; onEvents?: EChartEvents | undefined; actions?: ReactNode; controls?: ReactNode; height?: number | undefined; rowHref?: ((row: TabularRow) => string) | undefined }`
    (`ZoomMode = 'x' | 'y' | 'xy' | 'none'`; `defaultZoom` gives horizontal bars `'y'`). It renders a `Card` titled
    `title` (role `region` named `title`) whose header holds `actions` and three buttons named exactly `Table`, `CSV` and `Fullscreen`; the Table view is a `<table>`
    captioned `title`. Its view state lives in `useUrlState(urlKey, listCodec(enumCodec(['table', 'full'])), [])`, so
    **no other state on the page may use the same query key** (e.g. `?cal=table`).
  - `src/components/charts/ChartCard.tsx`:
    `ChartCard(props: { title: string; subtitle?: string | undefined; spec: QuerySpec; chartTypes: ChartType[]; allowedGroupBy?: Dim[] | undefined; allowedMetrics?: Metric[] | undefined; urlKey: string; drill?: ((row: TabularRow) => string) | undefined })`;
    `src/components/charts/explore.ts`: `type QuerySpec`, `type Dim`, `type Metric` (schema aliases) and
    `querySpec(input: QuerySpecInput): QuerySpec` with
    `type QuerySpecInput = Pick<QuerySpec, 'metric'> & Partial<Omit<QuerySpec, 'metric' | 'filters'>> & { filters?: Partial<Filters> }`
    (fills `agg`, `group_by`, `sort`, `limit` and every `filters` field with the server defaults).
  - Builders (`src/components/charts/builders/<name>.ts`, pure, each returns an `EChartsOption`):
    `lineOption(data: TabularData, opts: LineOpts)` with `interface LineOpts { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; band?: { lower: string; upper: string; name?: string }; markers?: { flag: string; name: string }; area?: boolean; smooth?: boolean; yName?: string; scaleY?: boolean }`;
    `barOption(data: TabularData, opts: BarOpts)` with `interface BarOpts { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; horizontal?: boolean; stack?: boolean; yName?: string; labels?: boolean }`;
    `heatmapOption(data: TabularData, opts: HeatmapOpts)` with `interface HeatmapOpts { x: string; y: string; value: string; min?: number; max?: number; labels?: boolean }`;
    `calendarOption(data: TabularData, opts: CalendarOpts)` with `interface CalendarOpts { date: string; value: string; year: number; min?: number; max?: number; orient?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical' }`;
    `ridgelineOption(data: TabularData, opts: RidgelineOpts)` with `interface RidgelineOpts { group: string; value: string; min?: number; max?: number; step?: number; bandwidth?: number; overlap?: number }`
    — **one row per observation** (one ridge per distinct `group`, a Gaussian kernel density of `value` over 0..50).
  - Tailwind utilities from the Plan 07 T1 `@theme` tokens: `bg-surface`, `bg-elevated`, `bg-primary`,
    `bg-primary-container`, `text-text`, `text-text-muted`, `text-accent`, `text-error`, `border-outline`,
    `border-outline-variant`, `rounded-card`, `rounded-button`, `rounded-sheet`.
  - e2e: `e2e/fixtures.ts` exports `test`, `expect`; Plan 04 T1's `e2e/authState.ts` exports
    `VIEWER_STATE = 'e2e/.auth/viewer.json'`, `ADMIN_STATE = 'e2e/.auth/admin.json'` and `e2ePassword(role)`; the
    `mobile`/`desktop` projects default to `storageState: VIEWER_STATE`; both files exist after the `setup` project
    (Plan 04 T1/T2), which also seeds both fixture workbooks. Admin specs opt in with
    `test.use({ storageState: ADMIN_STATE })` (Plan 04 D19).
- **D23 Fixture facts used by e2e and mocks** (computed from the fixtures with pandas/openpyxl, not guessed): 2026 has
  36 events, all scored; 2026-09-13 has 13 shooters (super sporting, Hadley station 36 vs score 34 is the only station
  mismatch; winner Nordquist, Sherman 42; mean 34.462, sample SD 4.807); 2026-09-27 has 23 shooters, head count 23 and a tie
  at 49 (Stockton, Ethan; Finnegan, Stanton); 2018-12-30 is attendance-only with 7; "Gilchrist, Melvin" is deceased with 11
  rounds (550 clays thrown, 409 broken); "Hamond, Bennett" (28 rounds, 2024-11-24 → 2026-09-27) and "Hammond, Bennett"
  (1 round, 2024-11-03) are a possible-duplicate pair with no shared date, and Plan 04 T3 orders each pair
  (smaller key, larger key), so `a` is Hammond (`'hammond bennett' < 'hamond bennett'`); "Hadley, Ike" has 267 rounds,
  2 wins, 14 podiums, average 35.31, median 36, best 45; after seeding, `incomplete_results` and
  `station_score_mismatch` each have exactly 1 data issue. Club facts (as of 2026-09-27): 311 scored and 310 held
  events, 7,480 rounds, 332 shooters (214 member, 109 guest, 9 deceased by current status), 261,461 clays broken, six
  50s, mean head count 22.56 over 360 counts; 2025 rounds by row status 1,210 member / 45 guest / 12 deceased, 2026
  915 / 54 / 0; newcomer cohorts 2025 = 57 (24 came back, 20 active in 2026) and 2026 = 41 (15 came back); first guest
  rounds 31 in 2025 and 29 in 2026, conversions 2 in 2025 (median 80.5 days) and 4 in 2026 (median 714 days); the
  trailing 364 days hold 48 held events, Abernathy attended 46 and Hadley 42, the only lapsed regular is "Nesbitt, Rolf"
  (last out 2026-05-31); winners 2025: 48 events, 23 different winners, top-3 share 0.3898, 2026: 36, 18, 0.3864;
  held events YTD 34 (2024), 35 (2025), 36 (2026).
- **D24 Local e2e runs are isolated per task** (Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 run concurrently in separate worktrees, then 3, 7
  and 8). Each task builds its own image tag (`TAG=08-1`, `08-2a`, `08-2b`, `08-3`, `08-4`, `08-5a`, `08-5b`, `08-5c`),
  runs Compose as project `sc-$TAG` with `IMAGE_TAG=$TAG`, and holds the machine-wide lock directory
  `/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime,
  because every stack publishes host port 8080. An `EXIT` trap runs `down -v` and removes the lock, so a failing RED
  run still releases it; the stack is never left running between steps. Images are built before the lock is taken.
  A lock with no `docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is stale (a crashed run): `rmdir` it.
- **D25 TDD order for `api.ts`.** A feature's response types and MSW fixtures come first (types emit no JavaScript;
  `mocks.ts` is test data), so `api.ts` starts as types only. Each TanStack hook is added in the GREEN step of the
  first failing test that renders a component calling it, never before.
- **D26 Recorded choices for review findings.** (a) Task 2 keeps C10's per-page "every data chart exposes Table and
  CSV controls" Vitest on the profile page (it asserts equal Table/CSV counts; the shell has none yet) and Task 3
  tightens the same test to exactly six charts, so C10's list, which names T2a, holds at every merge. (b)
  `DataIssueOut.details` and `AuditEntryOut.details` stay non-null: Plan 04 T3 declares `details: dict[str, Any]`
  (non-null in openapi.json) over C4 columns that Plan 03 T1 creates `NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'`, so Task 8 reads them
  directly and its mocks never use `details: null` (which would not type-check). A review asked for `details ?? {}` and
  a `details: null` test row on the premise that Plan 04 declares `dict[str, Any] | None`; Plan 04 as written does not,
  so the fallback would be dead code. Task 8's Step 0 checks the generated type and stops (BLOCKED) if it is nullable.
  (c) Club charts (Task 4) read every rolling mean from the server's `*_rolling8` columns (Decision D11), derive 1- and
  2-year retention from `CohortOut.retention` offsets 1 and 2, compute the conversion rate as
  `converted / new_guests` (null when a year has no new guests), and plot head count against score rows (`n_rounds`,
  spec §4 "count vs rows"), with shooters in the table.

  - `GET /api/events/{date}` (404 `event_not_found` for a date with no event) → `EventDetailOut = { event_date; round_type; round_type_source: string ('stations' | 'override' | 'none'); head_count: number | null; has_scores: boolean; has_stations: boolean; results_complete: boolean; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; median: number | null; mean: number | null; stdev: number | null; top_score: number | null; difficulty: number | null; results: EventResultOut[]; weather: EventWeatherOut | null; stations: StationMatrixOut | null; notables: NotableOut[]; vs_prev: VsPrevOut | null }` with
    - `EventResultOut = { round_id: number; shooter_id: number; display_name: string; name_key: string; shooter_status: string; ordinal: number; score: number; gauge_class: string | null; is_best_round: boolean; event_rank: number | null; percentile: number | null; adjusted: number | null; expected: number | null; residual: number | null; mu_before: number | null; mu_after: number | null; rating_delta: number | null }` (`name_key` per the D3 cross-plan fix; this task never reads it),
    - `EventWeatherOut = { temp_f; apparent_f; precip_in; wind_mph; gust_mph; wind_dir_deg; cloud_pct; humidity_pct; pressure_hpa: number | null; condition: string | null }`,
    - `StationMatrixOut = { layout: { station_no: number; target_count: number }[]; entries: { entry_row: number; name_key: string; shooter_id: number | null; display_name: string | null; round_id: number | null; hits: { station_no: number; hits: number }[]; total: number }[] }` (no recorded score: it comes from the linked result via `round_id`),
    - `NotableOut = { kind: 'pb' | 'first_timer' | 'upset'; shooter_id: number; display_name: string; detail: string; value: number | null }`,
    - `VsPrevOut = { prev_date: string; head_count_delta: number | null; median_delta: number | null; top_score_delta: number | null; difficulty_delta: number | null }`.

- [ ] **Step 0: Confirm the consumed Plan 07 names and the Plan 06 schema keys**

Run (from the worktree root):
```bash
cd frontend
for sym in renderWithProviders server api unwrap ApiError useUrlState intCodec useRoundTypes formatPressure ChartFrame heatmapOption TabularData NavItem Card Stat EmptyState Skeleton Button; do
  printf '%-20s %s\n' "$sym" "$(grep -rlE "export (default )?(async )?(function|const|class|type|interface) $sym\b" src | head -1)"
done
grep -nE "export (const|\{).*(test|expect)" e2e/fixtures.ts
grep -n "variant?: ButtonVariant\|description?: ReactNode" src/components/ui/Button.tsx src/components/ui/EmptyState.tsx
pnpm gen:api
grep -cF '"/api/events/{date}": {' src/api/schema.d.ts
awk '/ EventResultOut: \{/{f=1} f{print} f&&/^ *\};$/{exit}' src/api/schema.d.ts | grep -cF 'name_key: string;'
awk '/ StationEntryOut: \{/{f=1} f{print} f&&/^ *\};$/{exit}' src/api/schema.d.ts | grep -F 'hits:'
```
Expected: every symbol prints a file path; `e2e/fixtures.ts` exports `test` and `expect`; `Button` has
`variant?: ButtonVariant` and `EmptyState` has `description?: ReactNode`; the two counts print `1`; `StationEntryOut.hits`
is an array of `StationCellOut`. If a Plan 07 symbol is missing or its signature differs from **Consumes**, stop and
report NEEDS_CONTEXT with the actual export. If a count prints `0` (Plan 06 merged `{event_date}` or without
`EventResultOut.name_key`), stop and report BLOCKED: that is the Decision D3 cross-plan defect, fixed in Plan 06, not
here.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the e2e spec against a fresh stack to verify it fails**

Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 run concurrently in separate worktrees and every stack publishes host port 8080, so this task
builds its own image tag, runs Compose as its own project and holds the machine-wide lock directory
`/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime. The
`EXIT` trap tears the stack down and releases the lock even though this run fails. A lock with no
`docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is left over from a crashed run: `rmdir` it. Run from the
worktree root (`rm -rf secrets` deletes only this worktree's generated, gitignored dev secrets so the known e2e
passwords are used):
```bash
ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" && cd "$ROOT"
TAG=08-1
dc() { docker compose -p "sc-$TAG" -f "$ROOT/compose.yaml" -f "$ROOT/compose.test.yaml" "$@"; }
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-backend:$TAG" backend
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-frontend:$TAG" -f frontend/Dockerfile .
rm -rf secrets && VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin scripts/dev-secrets.sh
(cd frontend && pnpm exec playwright install chromium)
until mkdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock 2>/dev/null; do sleep 5; done
trap 'dc down -v; rmdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock' EXIT
IMAGE_TAG="$TAG" dc up -d --wait
(cd frontend && E2E_VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin pnpm exec playwright test e2e/events.spec.ts)
```
Expected: FAIL in both `mobile` and `desktop` — `getByRole('heading', { name: 'Events 2026' })` not found (no
`/events` route exists yet); then the trap removes the stack and the lock (`ls /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` → no such
file).

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `format.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/events/format.ts`:
```ts
const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  month: 'short',
  day: 'numeric',
  year: 'numeric',
  timeZone: 'UTC',
});

const ROUND_TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  sporting: 'Sporting',
  super_sporting: 'Super Sporting',
  unknown: 'Unknown',
};

/** '2026-09-13' → 'Sep 13, 2026'. Parsed as UTC midnight and formatted in UTC, so the day never shifts. */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

export function roundTypeLabel(roundType: string): string {
  return ROUND_TYPE_LABELS[roundType] ?? roundType;
}

/** +1.5 / −0.8 (U+2212) / 0.0; rounds before choosing the sign; null → '—'. */
export function formatSigned(value: number | null, digits = 1): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  const rounded = Number(value.toFixed(digits));
  if (rounded === 0) return (0).toFixed(digits);
  const magnitude = Math.abs(rounded).toFixed(digits);
  return rounded > 0 ? `+${magnitude}` : `−${magnitude}`;
}

export function formatScore(value: number | null): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
}

/** Published difficulty: positive = harder than a typical recent day (C7). */
export function difficultyLabel(difficulty: number | null): string {
  if (difficulty === null) return '—';
  const text = formatSigned(difficulty);
  if (text === '0.0') return '0.0 (typical)';
  return difficulty > 0 ? `${text} (harder than usual)` : `${text} (easier than usual)`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the section glob host**

Create `frontend/src/features/events/sections.ts`:
```ts
import type { FC } from 'react';

/** Extension point (C10): a feature exports `eventSection` from `src/features/<name>/eventSection.tsx`. */
export type EventSection = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  order: number;
  Component: FC<{ date: string }>;
};

type SectionModule = { eventSection?: EventSection };

export function collectEventSections(modules: Record<string, SectionModule>): EventSection[] {
  return Object.values(modules)
    .flatMap((m) => (m.eventSection ? [m.eventSection] : []))
    .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
}

export const eventSections: EventSection[] = collectEventSections(
  import.meta.glob<SectionModule>('../*/eventSection.tsx', { eager: true }),
);
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/components/EventSections.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { EventSection } from '../sections';

export function EventSections({ date, sections }: { date: string; sections: EventSection[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      {sections.map(({ id, title, Component }) => (
        <Card key={id} title={title}>
          <Component date={date} />
        </Card>
      ))}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Implement `charts.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/events/charts.ts`:
```ts
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import { heatmapOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/heatmap';
import type { TabularData } from '../../components/charts/types';
import type { EventResult, StationMatrix } from './api';

export type ChartModel = TabularData & { option: EChartsOption };

type Entry = StationMatrix['entries'][number];

/**
 * Pairs each entry with a display label: the matched shooter's name, else the sheet's name key (an unmatched
 * station name, C5). A repeated label gets " (2)", " (3)" so heatmap rows never collide.
 */
function labelled(entries: Entry[]): { entry: Entry; label: string }[] {
  const seen = new Map<string, number>();
  return entries.map((entry) => {
    const name = entry.display_name ?? entry.name_key;
    const n = (seen.get(name) ?? 0) + 1;
    seen.set(name, n);
    return { entry, label: n === 1 ? name : `${name} (${n})` };
  });
}

function hitsAt(entry: Entry, stationNo: number): number | null {
  return entry.hits.find((cell) => cell.station_no === stationNo)?.hits ?? null;
}

export function stationLongData(matrix: StationMatrix): TabularData {
  return {
    columns: [
      { key: 'shooter', label: 'Shooter', type: 'string' },
      { key: 'station', label: 'Station', type: 'string' },
      { key: 'hits', label: 'Hits', type: 'int' },
    ],
    rows: labelled(matrix.entries).flatMap(({ entry, label }) =>
      matrix.layout.map((s) => ({ shooter: label, station: `Stn ${s.station_no}`, hits: hitsAt(entry, s.station_no) })),
    ),
  };
}

/** Station table + heatmap; `results` supply the recorded score of the round each entry is linked to. */
export function stationMatrixModel(
  matrix: StationMatrix,
  results: readonly Pick<EventResult, 'round_id' | 'score'>[],
): ChartModel {
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'shooter', label: 'Shooter', type: 'string' },
    ...matrix.layout.map((s) => ({
      key: `s${s.station_no}`,
      label: `Stn ${s.station_no} (${s.target_count})`,
      type: 'int' as const,
    })),
    { key: 'total', label: 'Station total', type: 'int' },
    { key: 'score', label: 'Score', type: 'int' },
  ];
  const rows = labelled(matrix.entries).map(({ entry, label }) => {
    const row: TabularData['rows'][number] = { shooter: label };
    for (const s of matrix.layout) row[`s${s.station_no}`] = hitsAt(entry, s.station_no);
    row.total = entry.total;
    row.score = results.find((r) => entry.round_id !== null && r.round_id === entry.round_id)?.score ?? null;
    return row;
  });
  return {
    columns,
    rows,
    option: heatmapOption(stationLongData(matrix), { x: 'station', y: 'shooter', value: 'hits' }),
  };
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/components/ResultsTable.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { EventResult } from '../api';
import { formatSigned } from '../format';

/** Rank label per round: min-rank on best rounds ('T<n>' when shared), '—' on every other round (D8). */
export function rankLabels(results: EventResult[]): Map<number, string> {
  const shared = new Map<number, number>();
  for (const r of results) {
    if (r.is_best_round && r.event_rank !== null) shared.set(r.event_rank, (shared.get(r.event_rank) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  return new Map(
    results.map((r): [number, string] => {
      if (!r.is_best_round || r.event_rank === null) return [r.round_id, '—'];
      return [r.round_id, shared.get(r.event_rank) === 1 ? String(r.event_rank) : `T${r.event_rank}`];
    }),
  );
}

/** Decision D8: the server's rating_delta (mu_after − mu_before) on the best round only; null → '—'. */
export function ratingDelta(r: EventResult): string {
  return r.is_best_round ? formatSigned(r.rating_delta) : '—';
}

export function ResultsTable({ results }: { results: EventResult[] }) {
  const labels = rankLabels(results);
  const sorted = [...results].sort(
    (a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.display_name.localeCompare(b.display_name) || a.ordinal - b.ordinal,
  );
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table aria-label="Results" className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="text-left text-text-muted">
          <tr>
            <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2">Rank</th>
            <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2">Shooter</th>
            <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2 text-right">Score</th>
            <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2 text-right">Adj.</th>
            <th scope="col" className="py-2 text-right">Rating Δ</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {sorted.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.round_id} className="border-t border-outline-variant">
              <td className="py-2 pr-2 tabular-nums">{labels.get(r.round_id)}</td>
              <td className="py-2 pr-2">
                <Link to={`/shooters/${r.shooter_id}`} className="underline-offset-2 hover:underline">
                  {r.display_name}
                </Link>
                {r.ordinal > 1 && (
                  <span title="Additional round that day" className="ml-2 rounded-button border border-outline-variant px-2 text-xs">
                    R{r.ordinal}
                  </span>
                )}
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 pr-2 text-right tabular-nums">{r.score}</td>
              <td className="py-2 pr-2 text-right tabular-nums">{formatSigned(r.adjusted)}</td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{ratingDelta(r)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/components/WeatherCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { formatPressure } from '../../../lib/format';
import type { EventWeather } from '../api';

const CONDITION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  rain: 'Rain',
  windy: 'Windy',
  overcast: 'Overcast',
  partly_cloudy: 'Partly cloudy',
  clear: 'Clear',
};

const POINTS = ['N', 'NE', 'E', 'SE', 'S', 'SW', 'W', 'NW'] as const;

export function compass(deg: number): string {
  const normalized = ((deg % 360) + 360) % 360;
  // Index is always 0..7; the cast keeps this valid with or without noUncheckedIndexedAccess.
  return POINTS[Math.round(normalized / 45) % 8] as (typeof POINTS)[number];
}

function measure(value: number | null, unit: string, digits = 0): string {
  return value === null ? '—' : `${value.toFixed(digits)}${unit}`;
}

function windText(w: EventWeather): string {
  const direction = w.wind_dir_deg === null ? '' : ` ${compass(w.wind_dir_deg)}`;
  return `${measure(w.wind_mph, ' mph')}${direction}, gusts ${measure(w.gust_mph, ' mph')}`;
}

function Item({ label, value }: { label: string; value: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="tabular-nums">{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

export function WeatherCard({ weather }: { weather: EventWeather | null }) {
  return (
    <Card title="Weather (10:00–12:00)">
      {weather === null ? (
        <EmptyState title="No weather recorded for this event" />
      ) : (
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <Item label="Conditions" value={weather.condition === null ? '—' : (CONDITION_LABELS[weather.condition] ?? weather.condition)} />
          <Item label="Temperature" value={`${measure(weather.temp_f, '°F')} (feels ${measure(weather.apparent_f, '°F')})`} />
          <Item label="Wind" value={weather.wind_mph === null ? '—' : windText(weather)} />
          <Item label="Precipitation" value={measure(weather.precip_in, ' in', 2)} />
          <Item label="Cloud cover" value={measure(weather.cloud_pct, '%')} />
          <Item label="Humidity" value={measure(weather.humidity_pct, '%')} />
          <Item label="Pressure" value={weather.pressure_hpa === null ? '—' : formatPressure(weather.pressure_hpa)} />
        </dl>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 26: Implement the calendar and the list**

Create `frontend/src/features/events/components/SeasonCalendar.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { EventSummary } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

const MONTH = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' });

/** ISO dates of every Sunday in `year` (UTC arithmetic, so no DST or timezone drift). */
export function sundaysOfYear(year: number): string[] {
  const days: string[] = [];
  const d = new Date(Date.UTC(year, 0, 1));
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + ((7 - d.getUTCDay()) % 7));
  while (d.getUTCFullYear() === year) {
    days.push(d.toISOString().slice(0, 10));
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 7);
  }
  return days;
}

export function cellLabel(e: EventSummary): string {
  if (e.has_scores) return `${formatDay(e.event_date)} — ${e.n_shooters} shooters`;
  if (e.head_count !== null) return `${formatDay(e.event_date)} — attendance only, ${e.head_count} shooters`;
  return `${formatDay(e.event_date)} — no scores`;
}

export function SeasonCalendar({ year, events }: { year: number; events: EventSummary[] }) {
  const byDate = new Map(events.filter((e) => e.event_date.startsWith(`${year}-`)).map((e) => [e.event_date, e]));
  const days = [...new Set([...sundaysOfYear(year), ...byDate.keys()])].sort();
  const months = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => {
    const mm = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    return {
      key: mm,
      label: MONTH.format(new Date(Date.UTC(year, i, 1))),
      days: days.filter((d) => d.slice(5, 7) === mm),
    };
  });
  return (
    <section aria-label={`Season calendar ${year}`} className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-card bg-elevated p-3">
      {months.map((m) => (
        <div key={m.key} className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <span className="w-10 shrink-0 text-xs text-text-muted">{m.label}</span>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
            {m.days.map((day) => {
              const event = byDate.get(day);
              const dayOfMonth = Number(day.slice(8, 10));
              if (!event) {
                return (
                  <span key={day} aria-hidden="true" className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center text-sm opacity-40">
                    {dayOfMonth}
                  </span>
                );
              }
              return (
                <Link
                  key={day}
                  to={`/events/${day}`}
                  aria-label={cellLabel(event)}
                  className={`flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-button text-sm ${
                    event.has_scores ? 'bg-primary text-text' : 'border border-outline-variant text-text-muted'
                  }`}
                >
                  {dayOfMonth}
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/components/EventList.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { EventSummary } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatScore, roundTypeLabel } from '../format';

export function summaryLine(e: EventSummary): string {
  if (!e.has_scores) {
    return e.head_count === null ? 'No scores recorded' : `Attendance only · ${e.head_count} shooters`;
  }
  const parts = [`${e.n_shooters} shooters`, roundTypeLabel(e.round_type)];
  if (e.median !== null) parts.push(`median ${formatScore(e.median)}`);
  if (e.top_score !== null) parts.push(`top ${e.top_score}`);
  return parts.join(' · ');
}

export function EventList({ year, events }: { year: number; events: EventSummary[] }) {
  const sorted = [...events].sort((a, b) => b.event_date.localeCompare(a.event_date));
  return (
    <ol aria-label={`Events in ${year}`} className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-outline-variant rounded-card bg-elevated">
      {sorted.map((e) => (
        <li key={e.event_date}>
          <Link to={`/events/${e.event_date}`} className="flex min-h-11 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 px-4 py-2">
            <span className="font-medium">{formatDay(e.event_date)}</span>
            <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">{summaryLine(e)}</span>
          </Link>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';
```

```ts
export function useMeta() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/meta'],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/meta')),
  });
}

/** `year === null` waits (the season is not known yet). Appends the global round-type filter (C10). */
export function useEvents(year: number | null) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/events', { year, round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      // Only runs while enabled, i.e. with a non-null year.
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/events', { params: { query: { year: Number(year), round_type: roundTypes } } })),
    enabled: year !== null,
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/pages/EventsPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { intCodec, useUrlState } from '../../../lib/useUrlState';
import { useEvents, useMeta } from '../api';
import { EventList } from '../components/EventList';
import { SeasonCalendar } from '../components/SeasonCalendar';

function yearOf(iso: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  return iso ? Number(iso.slice(0, 4)) : null;
}

export function EventsPage() {
  const meta = useMeta();
  // intCodec: a missing or unreadable ?year= reads as 0, i.e. "the latest season".
  const [yearParam, setYear] = useUrlState('year', intCodec, 0);
  const lastYear = meta.isPending ? null : (yearOf(meta.data?.last_event_date) ?? new Date().getFullYear());
  const firstYear = yearOf(meta.data?.first_event_date) ?? lastYear;
  const year = yearParam || lastYear;
  const events = useEvents(year);

  if (year === null) return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;

  function body(season: number) {
    if (events.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
    if (events.isError) return <EmptyState title="Couldn't load events" description={events.error.message} />;
    if (events.data.length === 0) return <EmptyState title={`No events in ${season}`} />;
    return (
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <SeasonCalendar year={season} events={events.data} />
        <EventList year={season} events={events.data} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          aria-label="Previous season"
          disabled={firstYear !== null && year <= firstYear}
          onClick={() => setYear(year - 1)}
        >
          <ChevronLeft aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-medium">Events {year}</h1>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          aria-label="Next season"
          disabled={lastYear !== null && year >= lastYear}
          onClick={() => setYear(year + 1)}
        >
          <ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
      </header>
      {body(year)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import type { EventResult, StationMatrix } from '../api';
import { stationMatrixModel } from '../charts';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

export function StationHeatmap({
  stations,
  results,
  date,
}: {
  stations: StationMatrix | null;
  results: EventResult[];
  date: string;
}) {
  if (stations === null) {
    return (
      <Card title="Station hits">
        <EmptyState title="No station sheet for this event" />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  const model = stationMatrixModel(stations, results);
  return (
    <ChartFrame
      title="Station hits"
      subtitle="Hits per station (targets in brackets); station total vs recorded score in the table"
      option={model.option}
      columns={model.columns}
      rows={model.rows}
      csvName={`stations-${date}`}
      ariaLabel={`Station hits heatmap for ${formatDay(date)}`}
      urlKey="stn"
      zoom="none"
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import type { VsPrev } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatSigned } from '../format';

export function VsPrevCard({ vsPrev }: { vsPrev: VsPrev | null }) {
  return (
    <Card title="vs last week">
      {vsPrev === null ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">First event on record — nothing to compare.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">
            Compared with <Link to={`/events/${vsPrev.prev_date}`}>{formatDay(vsPrev.prev_date)}</Link>
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <Stat label="Head count" value={formatSigned(vsPrev.head_count_delta, 0)} />
            <Stat label="Median" value={formatSigned(vsPrev.median_delta)} />
            <Stat label="Top score" value={formatSigned(vsPrev.top_score_delta, 0)} />
            <Stat label="Difficulty" value={formatSigned(vsPrev.difficulty_delta)} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { Notable } from '../api';

// Plan 06 NotableOut.kind is 'pb' | 'first_timer' | 'upset'; any other kind is shown verbatim.
const KIND_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  pb: 'Personal best',
  first_timer: 'First time out',
  upset: 'Upset',
};

export function NotablesCard({ notables }: { notables: Notable[] }) {
  return (
    <Card title="Notables">
      {notables.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">Nothing notable this week.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {notables.map((n, i) => (
            <li key={`${n.kind}-${n.shooter_id}-${i}`}>
              <span className="font-medium">{KIND_LABELS[n.kind] ?? n.kind}</span>
              {': '}
              <Link to={`/shooters/${n.shooter_id}`}>{n.display_name}</Link>
              {` — ${n.detail}`}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/events/pages/EventDetailPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useParams } from 'react-router';
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/errors';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import { useEvent } from '../api';
import type { EventDetail } from '../api';
import { EventSections } from '../components/EventSections';
import { NotablesCard } from '../components/NotablesCard';
import { ResultsTable } from '../components/ResultsTable';
import { StationHeatmap } from '../components/StationHeatmap';
import { VsPrevCard } from '../components/VsPrevCard';
import { WeatherCard } from '../components/WeatherCard';
import { difficultyLabel, formatDay, formatScore, roundTypeLabel } from '../format';
import { eventSections } from '../sections';
import type { EventSection } from '../sections';

const SOURCE_NOTES: Record<string, string> = {
  stations: ' (from station sheets)',
  override: ' (admin override)',
};

export function attendanceOnlyTitle(headCount: number | null): string {
  return headCount === null
    ? 'No scores recorded for this event'
    : `Attendance only — ${headCount} shooters, no scores recorded`;
}

function ScoredEvent({ event }: { event: EventDetail }) {
  return (
    <>
      {!event.results_complete && event.head_count !== null && (
        <p role="note" className="rounded-card border border-accent p-3">
          {`Partial results — ${event.n_shooters} of ${event.head_count} shooters recorded.`}
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
        <Stat label="Shooters" value={String(event.n_shooters)} />
        <Stat label="Head count" value={event.head_count === null ? '—' : String(event.head_count)} />
        <Stat label="Median" value={formatScore(event.median)} />
        <Stat label="Top score" value={formatScore(event.top_score)} />
      </div>
      <Card title="Results">
        <ResultsTable results={event.results} />
      </Card>
      <StationHeatmap stations={event.stations} results={event.results} date={event.event_date} />
    </>
  );
}

function EventDetailView({ event, sections }: { event: EventDetail; sections: EventSection[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-medium">{formatDay(event.event_date)}</h1>
        <p className="text-text-muted">
          <span>{roundTypeLabel(event.round_type)}</span>
          {SOURCE_NOTES[event.round_type_source] ?? ''} · Difficulty {difficultyLabel(event.difficulty)}
        </p>
      </header>
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:col-span-2">
          {event.has_scores ? <ScoredEvent event={event} /> : <EmptyState title={attendanceOnlyTitle(event.head_count)} />}
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <WeatherCard weather={event.weather} />
          {event.has_scores && <VsPrevCard vsPrev={event.vs_prev} />}
          {event.has_scores && <NotablesCard notables={event.notables} />}
        </div>
      </div>
      <EventSections date={event.event_date} sections={sections} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function EventDetailPage({ sections = eventSections }: { sections?: EventSection[] }) {
  const { date = '' } = useParams();
  const event = useEvent(date);
  if (event.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
  if (event.isError) {
    const notFound = event.error instanceof ApiError && event.error.status === 404;
    return notFound ? (
      <EmptyState title={`No event on ${formatDay(date)}`} description="Pick a date from the events calendar." />
    ) : (
      <EmptyState title="Couldn't load this event" description={event.error.message} />
    );
  }
  return <EventDetailView event={event.data} sections={sections} />;
}
```

```tsx
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../test/msw/server';
import { meta } from './mocks';
import { routes } from './routes';

function renderAt(path: string) {
  // A pathless-looking parent with HydrateFallback mirrors the app's layout route, so the lazy children load
  // without React Router's "no HydrateFallback" warning.
  const router = createMemoryRouter([{ path: '/', HydrateFallback: () => null, children: routes }], {
    initialEntries: [path],
  });
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

// These tests use this feature's default handlers (./mocks, merged into the global MSW server) on purpose.
describe('events routes', () => {
  it('serve the season page at /events', async () => {
    server.use(http.get('*/api/meta', () => HttpResponse.json(meta))); // /api/meta's default handler is home's
    renderAt('/events');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Events 2026' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Events in 2026' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('serve the event page at /events/:date', async () => {
    renderAt('/events/2026-09-13');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Sep 13, 2026' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `format.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/format.ts`:
```ts
const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });
const MONTH = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'long', timeZone: 'UTC' });

/** '2020-01-05' → 'Jan 5, 2020' (UTC in, UTC out: the day never shifts). */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

export function spanText(first: string | null, last: string | null): string {
  if (first === null || last === null) return '—';
  return first === last ? formatDay(first) : `${formatDay(first)} – ${formatDay(last)}`;
}

/** +3.4 / −2.1 (U+2212) / 0.0; rounds before choosing the sign; null → '—'. */
export function formatSigned(value: number | null, digits = 1): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  const rounded = Number(value.toFixed(digits));
  if (rounded === 0) return (0).toFixed(digits);
  const magnitude = Math.abs(rounded).toFixed(digits);
  return rounded > 0 ? `+${magnitude}` : `−${magnitude}`;
}

export function formatScore(value: number | null): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
}

/** Fraction 0–1 → whole percent. */
export function formatPct(fraction: number | null): string {
  return fraction === null ? '—' : `${Math.round(fraction * 100)}%`;
}

export function monthName(month: number): string {
  return MONTH.format(new Date(Date.UTC(2000, month - 1, 1)));
}

/** Plan 06 T8: form = mean residual of the last 5 rounds; hot ≥ +3, cold ≤ −3. */
export function formLabel(form: number | null): string {
  if (form === null) return '—';
  if (form >= 3) return 'Hot';
  if (form <= -3) return 'Cold';
  return 'Steady';
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the profile-section glob host**

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/sections.ts`:
```ts
import type { FC } from 'react';

/** Extension point (C10): a feature exports `profileSection` from `src/features/<name>/profileSection.tsx`. */
export type ProfileSection = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  order: number;
  Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }>;
};

type SectionModule = { profileSection?: ProfileSection };

export function collectProfileSections(modules: Record<string, SectionModule>): ProfileSection[] {
  return Object.values(modules)
    .flatMap((m) => (m.profileSection ? [m.profileSection] : []))
    .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
}

export const profileSections: ProfileSection[] = collectProfileSections(
  import.meta.glob<SectionModule>('../*/profileSection.tsx', { eager: true }),
);
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/ProfileSections.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { ProfileSection } from '../sections';

export function ProfileSections({ shooterId, sections }: { shooterId: number; sections: ProfileSection[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      {sections.map(({ id, title, Component }) => (
        <Card key={id} title={title}>
          <Component shooterId={shooterId} />
        </Card>
      ))}
    </>
  );
}
```

```ts
import type { paths } from '../../api/schema';

export type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } } ? R : never;

export type ShooterListItem = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters']['get']>[number];
export type ShooterDetail = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters/{id}']['get']>;
export type Odometer = ShooterDetail['odometer'];
export type PersonalBest = ShooterDetail['pbs'][number];
export type ShooterInsights = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters/{id}/insights']['get']>;
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Implement `MeButton` and `OdometerStrip`**

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/MeButton.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { clearMe, getMe, setMe } from '../../../lib/me';

/** "That's me" personalization (spec §4): stored in localStorage via lib/me. */
export function MeButton({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const [me, setMeState] = useState<number | null>(() => getMe());
  const isMe = me === shooterId;
  function toggle() {
    if (isMe) {
      clearMe();
      setMeState(null);
    } else {
      setMe(shooterId);
      setMeState(shooterId);
    }
  }
  return (
    <Button variant={isMe ? 'primary' : 'tonal'} aria-pressed={isMe} onClick={toggle}>
      {isMe ? 'This is you' : "That's me"}
    </Button>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/OdometerStrip.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Odometer } from '../api';
import { monthName } from '../format';

export function odometerItems(o: Odometer): { label: string; value: string }[] {
  const count = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString('en-US');
  return [
    { label: 'Clays thrown', value: count(o.clays_thrown) },
    { label: 'Clays broken', value: count(o.clays_broken) },
    { label: 'Hit rate', value: o.clays_thrown > 0 ? `${((100 * o.clays_broken) / o.clays_thrown).toFixed(1)}%` : '—' },
    { label: 'Rounds', value: count(o.rounds) },
    { label: 'Events', value: count(o.events) },
    { label: 'Years active', value: count(o.years_active) },
    { label: 'Current streak', value: count(o.current_streak) },
    { label: 'Longest streak', value: count(o.longest_streak) },
    { label: 'Favorite month', value: o.favorite_month === null ? '—' : monthName(o.favorite_month) },
    { label: 'Trophies', value: count(o.trophies) },
  ];
}

export function OdometerStrip({ odometer }: { odometer: Odometer }) {
  return (
    <ul aria-label="Lifetime odometer" className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 rounded-card bg-elevated p-3 sm:grid-cols-5">
      {odometerItems(odometer).map((item) => (
        <li key={item.label} className="flex flex-col">
          <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">{item.label}</span>
          <span className="text-lg tabular-nums">{item.value}</span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
export function InfoItem({ label, value, hint }: { label: string; value: ReactNode; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">{label}</dt>
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/InsightsCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { useShooterInsights } from '../api';
import type { ShooterInsights } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatPct, formatSigned, formLabel } from '../format';
import { InfoItem } from './InfoItem';

export function rustText(rust: ShooterInsights['rust']): string {
  if (rust.effect === null) return 'No layoffs yet';
  return `${formatSigned(rust.effect)} after 6+ week layoffs (n=${rust.n}; club ${formatSigned(rust.club_effect)})`;
}

/** Plan 06 T8 milestone: the next C12 `events` tier; `next_events` is null once 250 events are passed. */
export function milestoneText(m: ShooterInsights['milestone']): string {
  if (m.next_events === null || m.events_to_go === null) return 'All milestones reached';
  const base = `${m.next_events} events — ${m.events_to_go} to go`;
  return m.projected_date === null ? base : `${base}, projected ${formatDay(m.projected_date)}`;
}

export function peakText(mu: number | null, date: string | null): string {
  if (mu === null) return '—';
  return date === null ? mu.toFixed(1) : `${mu.toFixed(1)} (${formatDay(date)})`;
}

function InsightsGrid({ insights: i }: { insights: ShooterInsights }) {
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <InfoItem
        label="Floor / ceiling"
        value={i.floor === null || i.ceiling === null ? '—' : `${i.floor.toFixed(1)} / ${i.ceiling.toFixed(1)}`}
        hint="P10 / P90 of the last 20 rounds"
      />
      <InfoItem label="Bad-day rate" value={formatPct(i.bad_day_rate)} hint="Rounds 6+ below expectation" />
      <InfoItem
        label="Form"
        value={i.form === null ? '—' : `${formLabel(i.form)} (${formatSigned(i.form)})`}
        hint="Vs expectation, last 5 rounds"
      />
      <InfoItem label="Rust" value={rustText(i.rust)} hint="First round back vs other rounds" />
      <InfoItem label="Wins / podiums" value={`${i.wins} / ${i.podiums}`} />
      <InfoItem label="Avg percentile" value={formatPct(i.avg_percentile)} />
      <InfoItem label="Peak rating" value={peakText(i.peak_mu, i.peak_date)} />
      <InfoItem label="Next milestone" value={milestoneText(i.milestone)} />
    </dl>
  );
}

export function InsightsCard({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const insights = useShooterInsights(shooterId);
  let body: ReactNode;
  if (insights.isPending) body = <Skeleton className="h-32" />;
  else if (insights.isError) body = <p className="text-text-muted">Insights unavailable right now.</p>;
  else body = <InsightsGrid insights={insights.data} />;
  return <Card title="Insights">{body}</Card>;
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { clearMe, setMe } from '../../../lib/me';

import { renderWithProviders } from '../../../test/render';
import { shooterList } from '../mocks';
import { ShootersPage } from './ShootersPage';

const requests: URLSearchParams[] = [];

describe('ShootersPage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    clearMe();
    requests.length = 0;
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/shooters', ({ request }) => {
        const params = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
        requests.push(params);
        const q = (params.get('q') ?? '').toLowerCase();
        return HttpResponse.json(shooterList.filter((s) => s.display_name.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
      }),
    );
  });

    const list = await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Shooters' });
    expect(within(list).getAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(4);
    const gilchrist = within(list).getByRole('link', { name: /Gilchrist, Melvin/ });

    expect(within(gilchrist).getByRole('img', { name: 'In memoriam' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(within(list).getByRole('link', { name: /Mortlock, Beatrice/ })).getByText('Guest')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(requests[0]?.has('active')).toBe(false);
```
