    await user.type(await screen.findByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search shooters' }), 'gilchrist');
    // Every keystroke is a new (uncached) query that shows the skeleton, so the next list is the 'gilchrist' result.

    await waitFor(() => expect(requests.at(-1)?.get('active')).toBe('true'));
  });

  it('marks the remembered shooter as you', async () => {
    setMe(3);
    renderWithProviders(<ShootersPage />, { route: '/shooters' });
    const hadley = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /Hadley, Ike/ });
    expect(within(hadley).getByText('You')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when nothing matches the search', async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<ShootersPage />, { route: '/shooters?q=zzz' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('No shooters match “zzz”')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when there are no shooters at all', async () => {
    server.use(http.get('*/api/shooters', () => HttpResponse.json([])));
    renderWithProviders(<ShootersPage />, { route: '/shooters' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('No shooters yet')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows a load failure', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/shooters', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'Internal server error' } }, { status: 500 }),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<ShootersPage />, { route: '/shooters' });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Couldn't load shooters")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

export function useShooters(q: string, active: boolean) {

    queryKey: ['/api/shooters', { q, active }],

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/pages/ShootersPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Ribbon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useDeferredValue } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Toggle } from '../../../components/ui/Toggle';
import { getMe } from '../../../lib/me';
import { boolCodec, stringCodec, useUrlState } from '../../../lib/useUrlState';
import { useShooters } from '../api';
import type { ShooterListItem } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

function ShooterRow({ shooter, isMe }: { shooter: ShooterListItem; isMe: boolean }) {
  return (
    <li>
      <Link to={`/shooters/${shooter.shooter_id}`} className="flex min-h-11 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 px-4 py-2">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="font-medium">{shooter.display_name}</span>
          {shooter.status === 'deceased' && (
            <span role="img" aria-label="In memoriam" className="text-accent">
              <Ribbon aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
            </span>
          )}
          {shooter.status === 'guest' && <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">Guest</span>}
          {isMe && <span className="rounded-button bg-primary px-2 text-xs">You</span>}
        </span>
        <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">
          {shooter.n_events} events{shooter.last_event ? ` · last ${formatDay(shooter.last_event)}` : ''}
        </span>
      </Link>
    </li>
  );
}

export function ShootersPage() {
  // Decision D19: search text and the Active-only toggle live in the URL (?q=, ?active=1).
  const [q, setQ] = useUrlState('q', stringCodec, '');
  const [active, setActive] = useUrlState('active', boolCodec, false);
  const search = useDeferredValue(q).trim();
  const shooters = useShooters(search, active);
  const me = getMe();

  let body: ReactNode;
  if (shooters.isPending) body = <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
  else if (shooters.isError) body = <EmptyState title="Couldn't load shooters" description={shooters.error.message} />;
  else if (shooters.data.length === 0)
    body = <EmptyState title={search ? `No shooters match “${search}”` : 'No shooters yet'} />;
  else
    body = (
      <ul aria-label="Shooters" className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-outline-variant rounded-card bg-elevated">
        {shooters.data.map((s) => (
          <ShooterRow key={s.shooter_id} shooter={s} isMe={s.shooter_id === me} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    );

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-medium">Shooters</h1>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <input
          type="search"
          aria-label="Search shooters"
          placeholder="Search by name"
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          className="min-h-11 flex-1 rounded-button border border-outline-variant bg-elevated px-4"
        />
        <Toggle checked={active} onChange={setActive} label="Active only" />
      </div>
      {body}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Add `import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';` to the imports of `frontend/src/features/shooters/api.ts`

```ts
/** Detail + odometer; appends the global round-type filter (C10). Disabled for a non-positive or non-integer id. */
export function useShooter(id: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}', id, { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}', { params: { path: { id }, query: { round_type: roundTypes } } })),
    enabled: Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0,
  });
}
```

```tsx
import { Ribbon } from 'lucide-react';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import type { ShooterDetail } from '../api';
import { formatScore, spanText } from '../format';
import { MeButton } from './MeButton';

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<string, string | undefined> = { member: 'Member', guest: 'Guest' };

export function ProfileHero({ shooter }: { shooter: ShooterDetail }) {
  // 'deceased' has no label: the memorial marker (Plan 06 `deceased`) covers it.
  const statusLabel = STATUS_LABELS[shooter.status];
  const { stats } = shooter;
  return (
    <header className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-card bg-elevated p-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-medium">{shooter.display_name}</h1>
          {shooter.deceased && (
            <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-accent">
              <Ribbon aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
              In memoriam
            </p>
          )}
          <p className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm text-text-muted">
            {statusLabel && <span>{statusLabel}</span>}
            <span>{spanText(shooter.first_event, shooter.last_event)}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <MeButton shooterId={shooter.shooter_id} />
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5">
        <Stat label="Rounds" value={String(stats.n_rounds)} />
        <Stat label="Events" value={String(stats.n_events)} />
        <Stat label="Average" value={formatScore(stats.avg_score)} />
        <Stat label="Median" value={formatScore(stats.median_score)} />
        <Stat label="Best" value={formatScore(stats.best_score)} />
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/pages/ProfilePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useParams } from 'react-router';
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/errors';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { useShooter } from '../api';
import { InsightsCard } from '../components/InsightsCard';
import { OdometerStrip } from '../components/OdometerStrip';
import { ProfileHero } from '../components/ProfileHero';
import { ProfileSections } from '../components/ProfileSections';
import { profileSections } from '../sections';
import type { ProfileSection } from '../sections';

export function ProfilePage({ sections = profileSections }: { sections?: ProfileSection[] }) {
  const { id: idParam = '' } = useParams();
  const id = Number(idParam);
  const shooter = useShooter(id);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return <EmptyState title="Shooter not found" description="Pick someone from the shooters directory." />;
  }
  if (shooter.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
  if (shooter.isError) {
    return shooter.error instanceof ApiError && shooter.error.status === 404 ? (
      <EmptyState title="Shooter not found" description="They may have been merged into another name." />
    ) : (
      <EmptyState title="Couldn't load this shooter" description={shooter.error.message} />
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <ProfileHero shooter={shooter.data} />
      <OdometerStrip odometer={shooter.data.odometer} />
      <InsightsCard shooterId={id} />
      <ProfileSections shooterId={id} sections={sections} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { routes } from './routes';

function renderAt(path: string) {
  const router = createMemoryRouter([{ path: '/', HydrateFallback: () => null, children: routes }], {
    initialEntries: [path],
  });
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

});

### Task 3: Profile charts — rating, trend, distribution, learning curve, splits, attendance, PBs (master Plan 08 T2b)

**Branch:** `task/08-2b-profile-charts` (stacked on `task/08-2a-shooters`) · **Depends on:** Task 2 (T2a), Plan 06 T6
(`/rounds`, `/rating`, `/splits`), Plan 06 T7 (`/api/club/distribution`), Plan 06 T8 (`/insights` learning curve),
Plan 07 T5–T6 (builders, `ChartFrame`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/shooters/api.ts` (append types in Step 3, hooks in Step 14)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/shooters/mocks.ts` (append fixtures + handlers)
- Create: `frontend/src/features/shooters/charts.ts`, `charts.test.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/QueryChart.tsx`, `QueryChart.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/ProfileCharts.tsx`, `ProfileCharts.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/PbTable.tsx`, `PbTable.test.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/shooters/pages/ProfilePage.tsx` (render charts + PB table)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/shooters/pages/ProfilePage.test.tsx` (pin the chart endpoints; tighten the C10 test)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/shooters/routes.test.tsx` (pin `/api/club/distribution`, which the club feature owns)
- Create: `frontend/e2e/shooter-charts.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 2, `features/shooters/`): `export type JsonOf<Op>`; `useShooterInsights(id: number)`;
  `type ShooterInsights`, `type PersonalBest` (= Plan 06 `PbOut { scope: 'overall' | 'year'; key: string; score: number; event_date: string; round_id: number }`);
  `ProfilePage({ sections?: ProfileSection[] })` and its test `every data chart exposes Table and CSV controls`;
  `formatDay(iso: string): string`; mocks `hadleyDetail`, `hadleyInsights`, `handlers`.
- Consumes (Plan 07, verbatim — Decision D22):
  - `src/components/charts/ChartFrame.tsx`: `ChartFrame({ title: string; subtitle?: string; option: EChartsOption; columns: TabularData['columns']; rows: TabularData['rows']; csvName: string; ariaLabel: string; urlKey: string; zoom?: 'x' | 'y' | 'xy' | 'none'; onEvents?; actions?: ReactNode; controls?: ReactNode; height?: number; rowHref?: (row: TabularRow) => string })`
    — a `Card` titled `title` (role `region`) with buttons named exactly `Table`, `CSV`, `Fullscreen`; the Table view is a
    `<table>` captioned `title`; its view state is stored in the `urlKey` query parameter as `table`/`full`, so no
    other state may use that key.
  - `src/components/charts/types.ts`: `interface TabularData { columns: { key: string; label: string; type: 'date' | 'number' | 'string' | 'int' }[]; rows: Record<string, string | number | null>[] }`.
  - Builders: `lineOption(data: TabularData, opts: { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; band?: { lower: string; upper: string; name?: string }; markers?: { flag: string; name: string }; area?: boolean; smooth?: boolean; yName?: string; scaleY?: boolean }): EChartsOption`;
    `barOption(data: TabularData, opts: { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; horizontal?: boolean; stack?: boolean; yName?: string; labels?: boolean }): EChartsOption`;
    `calendarOption(data: TabularData, opts: { date: string; value: string; year: number; min?: number; max?: number; orient?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical' }): EChartsOption`.
  - `src/lib/useUrlState.ts`: `useUrlState<T>(key: string, codec: Codec<T>, defaultValue: T): [T, (next: T) => void]`,
    `intCodec: Codec<number>`, `enumCodec<T extends string>(values: readonly T[]): Codec<T>` (unknown value → default).
  - `src/lib/roundTypes.ts`: `useRoundTypes(): [RoundTypeValue[], (next: RoundTypeValue[]) => void]`.
  - `src/components/ui/`: `Card({ title?: ReactNode; …; children?: ReactNode })`,
    `Chip({ children: ReactNode; selected?: boolean; onClick?: () => void; … })` (`<button aria-pressed>`),
    `EmptyState({ title: string; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode })`,
    `Skeleton({ label?: string; lines?: number; className?: string })`.
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `renderWithProviders(ui, options?: { route?: string; path?: string; … }): ProvidersResult`
    (includes `router`); `src/test/msw/server.ts`: `server`.
- Consumes (Plan 06 response models as written; C8 path templates such as `/api/events/{date}` and `/api/shooters/{id}`,
  which Plan 06 binds with `Path(alias=…)` — never rename them here):
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}/rounds?round_type=` → `ShooterRoundOut[]` (date, ordinal ascending), `ShooterRoundOut = { round_id: number; event_date: string; ordinal: number; score: number; gauge_class: string | null; status: string | null; round_type: 'sporting' | 'super_sporting' | 'unknown'; is_best_round: boolean; event_rank: number | null; percentile: number | null; field_median: number | null; adjusted: number | null; expected: number | null; residual: number | null; mu_before: number | null; mu_after: number | null; condition: string | null }`.
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}/rating` → `RatingOut = { shooter_id: number; points: { event_date: string; mu: number; var: number; lo: number; hi: number }[]; current_mu: number | null; peak_mu: number | null; peak_date: string | null }` (published scale; `lo`/`hi` = mu ∓ 1.96·√var, C7).
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}/splits?by=<season|month|year|round_type|gauge|temp_band|wind_band|precip_band>&round_type=` → `SplitOut[]`, `SplitOut = { key: string; n_rounds: number; n_events: number; avg: number; median: number; best: number; avg_adjusted: number | null }` (`best` = the PB for that group).
  - `GET /api/club/distribution?by=year&round_type=` → `DistributionOut[]`, `DistributionOut = { key: string; n: number; mean: number; median: number; p10: number; p25: number; p75: number; p90: number; counts: number[] }` (`counts[s]` = rounds scoring `s`, length 51).
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}/insights` → `learning_curve: { k: number; value: number; club_median: number | null; n_club: number }[]` (Task 2's `ShooterInsights`).
- MSW rule (Decision D4): `shooters/mocks.ts` owns the viewer `*/api/shooters*` handlers; `/api/club/*` belongs to
  `features/club`, so every test that renders the distribution chart pins `*/api/club/distribution` with `server.use`.
- Produces: `features/shooters/api.ts` adds `useShooterRounds(id: number)`, `useShooterRating(id: number)`,
  `useShooterSplits(id: number, by: SplitBy)`, `useClubDistribution()`, types `ShooterRound`, `Rating`, `RatingPoint`,
  `SplitRow`, `SplitBy`, `ClubDistributionGroup`; `features/shooters/charts.ts`: `ChartModel`, `ratingModel`,
  `pbRoundIds`, `trendModel`, `distributionModel`, `learningCurveModel`, `splitsModel`, `activeYears`,
  `attendanceModel`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the e2e spec against a fresh stack to verify it fails**

Tasks 3, 7 and 8 run concurrently in separate worktrees and every stack publishes host port 8080, so this task
builds its own image tag, runs Compose as its own project and holds the machine-wide lock directory
`/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime. The
`EXIT` trap tears the stack down and releases the lock even though this run fails. A lock with no
`docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is left over from a crashed run: `rmdir` it. Run from the
worktree root (`rm -rf secrets` deletes only this worktree's generated, gitignored dev secrets so the known e2e
passwords are used):
```bash
ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" && cd "$ROOT"
TAG=08-2b
dc() { docker compose -p "sc-$TAG" -f "$ROOT/compose.yaml" -f "$ROOT/compose.test.yaml" "$@"; }
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-backend:$TAG" backend
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-frontend:$TAG" -f frontend/Dockerfile .
rm -rf secrets && VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin scripts/dev-secrets.sh
(cd frontend && pnpm exec playwright install chromium)
until mkdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock 2>/dev/null; do sleep 5; done
trap 'dc down -v; rmdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock' EXIT
IMAGE_TAG="$TAG" dc up -d --wait
(cd frontend && E2E_VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin pnpm exec playwright test e2e/shooter-charts.spec.ts)
```
Expected: FAIL in `mobile` and `desktop` — text "Rating" / group "Split by" not found (the profile has no charts yet);
then the trap removes the stack and the lock.

// Every Plan 06 ShooterRoundOut field; only event_date, ordinal, score, adjusted and round_id matter here.
function round(over: Partial<ShooterRound>): ShooterRound {
  return {
    round_id: 1,
    event_date: '2026-01-04',

}

// Seven dates; 2026-02-08 has two equal rounds listed ordinal 2 first.
const history: ShooterRound[] = [
  round({ round_id: 1, event_date: '2026-01-04', score: 30, adjusted: -5 }),
  round({ round_id: 2, event_date: '2026-01-11', score: 32, adjusted: -3 }),

  round({ round_id: 8, event_date: '2026-02-15', score: 38, adjusted: null }),
];

describe('pbRoundIds', () => {

    expect([...pbRoundIds(history)].sort()).toEqual([7, 8]);
  });

describe('trendModel', () => {
  it('orders rounds by date then ordinal, flags PBs and pins them on the score series', () => {
    const model = trendModel([...history].reverse());
    expect(model.rows.map((r) => [r.event_date, r.score, r.pb])).toEqual([
      ['2026-01-04', 30, ''],

      ['2026-02-08', 37, ''],

    ]);

    expect(score?.markPoint?.data.map((d) => d.coord)).toEqual([
      [5, 37],
      [7, 38],
    ]);
    expect(adjusted?.data).toEqual([-5, -3, -4, -2, 0, 2, 2, null]);
  });
});

describe('ratingModel', () => {
  // lo/hi are the server's mu ∓ 1.96·√var, hand-computed: ±3.92 for var 4, ±3.528 for var 3.24.
  const points: RatingPoint[] = [
    { event_date: '2026-09-06', mu: 35.3, var: 4, lo: 31.38, hi: 39.22 },

    expect(rating?.markPoint?.data[0]?.coord).toEqual(['2026-09-27', 35.4]);

  it('has no peak pin without points', () => {

describe('distributionModel', () => {
  const counts = (entries: Record<number, number>) => Array.from({ length: 51 }, (_, s) => entries[s] ?? 0);
  // Only `counts` feed the model; the other DistributionOut fields are set because they are required.
  const groups: ClubDistributionGroup[] = [
    { key: '2025', n: 4, mean: 40.75, median: 40.5, p10: 40, p25: 40, p75: 41.25, p90: 41.7, counts: counts({ 40: 2, 41: 1, 42: 1 }) },
    { key: '2026', n: 2, mean: 45.5, median: 45.5, p10: 41.9, p25: 43.25, p75: 47.75, p90: 49.1, counts: counts({ 41: 1, 50: 1 }) },
  ];

  it('compares the shooter score shares with the club shares from the lowest score seen up to 50', () => {
    const model = distributionModel(
      [round({ score: 40 }), round({ round_id: 2, score: 40 }), round({ round_id: 3, score: 42 })],
      groups,
    );
    expect(model.rows[0]).toEqual({ score: 40, you_pct: 66.7, club_pct: 33.3 });
    expect(model.rows[1]).toEqual({ score: 41, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 33.3 });
    expect(model.rows[2]).toEqual({ score: 42, you_pct: 33.3, club_pct: 16.7 });
    expect(model.rows.at(-1)).toEqual({ score: 50, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 16.7 });
    expect(model.rows).toHaveLength(11);
  });

  it('shows zero shares for a shooter with no rounds in the current filter', () => {
    expect(distributionModel([], groups).rows[0]).toEqual({ score: 40, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 33.3 });
  });

  it('shows zero club shares when the club distribution is empty', () => {
    expect(distributionModel([round({ score: 45 })], []).rows).toEqual([
      { score: 45, you_pct: 100, club_pct: 0 },
      { score: 46, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 0 },
      { score: 47, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 0 },
      { score: 48, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 0 },
      { score: 49, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 0 },
      { score: 50, you_pct: 0, club_pct: 0 },
    ]);
  });

  it('is empty when nobody has a round', () => {
    expect(distributionModel([], []).rows).toEqual([]);
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `charts.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/charts.ts`:
```ts
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import { barOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/bar';
import { calendarOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/calendar';
import { lineOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/line';
import type { TabularData } from '../../components/charts/types';
import type { ClubDistributionGroup, RatingPoint, ShooterInsights, ShooterRound, SplitRow } from './api';

export type ChartModel = TabularData & { option: EChartsOption };

const round1 = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 10) / 10;

/** Rating on the published scale with the server's ±1.96·√var band `lo..hi` (C7) and a pin on the peak (Decision D12). */
export function ratingModel(points: RatingPoint[]) {
  const rows = points.map((p) => ({ event_date: p.event_date, rating: round1(p.mu), low: round1(p.lo), high: round1(p.hi) }));
  let peak: (typeof rows)[number] | null = null;
  for (const r of rows) if (peak === null || r.rating > peak.rating) peak = r;
  const option: EChartsOption = {
    tooltip: { trigger: 'axis' },
    legend: { data: ['Rating', '95% band'] },
    xAxis: { type: 'category', boundaryGap: false, data: rows.map((r) => r.event_date) },
    yAxis: { type: 'value', scale: true, name: 'Rating' },
    series: [
      { name: 'band-low', type: 'line', stack: 'band', symbol: 'none', lineStyle: { opacity: 0 }, silent: true, data: rows.map((r) => r.low) },
      { name: '95% band', type: 'line', stack: 'band', symbol: 'none', lineStyle: { opacity: 0 }, areaStyle: { opacity: 0.25 }, data: rows.map((r) => round1(r.high - r.low)) },
      {
        name: 'Rating',
        type: 'line',
        symbol: 'none',
        data: rows.map((r) => r.rating),
        markPoint: peak === null ? undefined : { data: [{ name: 'Peak', coord: [peak.event_date, peak.rating], value: peak.rating }] },
      },
    ],
  };
  return {
    columns: [
      { key: 'event_date', label: 'Date', type: 'date' },
      { key: 'rating', label: 'Rating', type: 'number' },
      { key: 'low', label: 'Band low', type: 'number' },
      { key: 'high', label: 'Band high', type: 'number' },
    ] satisfies TabularData['columns'],
    rows,
    option,
  };
}

/** C12 personal_bests rule (Decision D10): at most one PB per date, the lowest-ordinal top round. */
export function pbRoundIds(rounds: ShooterRound[]): Set<number> {
  const byDate = new Map<string, ShooterRound[]>();
  for (const r of rounds) byDate.set(r.event_date, [...(byDate.get(r.event_date) ?? []), r]);
  const pbs = new Set<number>();
  let best = -Infinity;
  let earlier = 0;
  for (const date of [...byDate.keys()].sort()) {
    const day = byDate.get(date) as ShooterRound[];
    const top = day.reduce((a, b) => (b.score > a.score || (b.score === a.score && b.ordinal < a.ordinal) ? b : a));
    if (earlier >= 5 && top.score > best) pbs.add(top.round_id);
    best = Math.max(best, top.score);
    earlier += day.length;
  }
  return pbs;
}

/** Score (left axis, 0–50) and field-adjusted score (right axis) per round, PB pins on the score line. */
export function trendModel(rounds: ShooterRound[]) {
  const sorted = [...rounds].sort((a, b) => a.event_date.localeCompare(b.event_date) || a.ordinal - b.ordinal);
  const pbs = pbRoundIds(sorted);
  const rows = sorted.map((r) => ({
    event_date: r.event_date,
    score: r.score,
    adjusted: r.adjusted,
    pb: pbs.has(r.round_id) ? 'PB' : '',
  }));
  const option: EChartsOption = {
    tooltip: { trigger: 'axis' },
    legend: { data: ['Score', 'Adjusted'] },
    xAxis: { type: 'category', data: rows.map((r) => r.event_date) },
    yAxis: [
      { type: 'value', name: 'Score', min: 0, max: 50 },
      { type: 'value', name: 'Adjusted' },
    ],
    series: [
      {
        name: 'Score',
        type: 'line',
        symbolSize: 4,
        data: rows.map((r) => r.score),
        markPoint: {
          symbol: 'pin',
          data: rows.flatMap((r, i) => (r.pb ? [{ name: 'PB', coord: [i, r.score], value: r.score }] : [])),
        },
      },
      { name: 'Adjusted', type: 'line', yAxisIndex: 1, symbolSize: 4, data: rows.map((r) => r.adjusted) },
    ],
  };
  return {
    columns: [
      { key: 'event_date', label: 'Date', type: 'date' },
      { key: 'score', label: 'Score', type: 'int' },
      { key: 'adjusted', label: 'Adjusted', type: 'number' },
      { key: 'pb', label: 'PB', type: 'string' },
    ] satisfies TabularData['columns'],
    rows,
    option,
  };
}

function share(count: number, total: number): number {
  return total === 0 ? 0 : round1((100 * count) / total);
}

/** The shooter's score shares vs the club's all-years shares (Decision D11). */
export function distributionModel(rounds: ShooterRound[], groups: ClubDistributionGroup[]): ChartModel {
  const you = new Map<number, number>();
  for (const r of rounds) you.set(r.score, (you.get(r.score) ?? 0) + 1);
  const club = new Map<number, number>();
  for (const g of groups) g.counts.forEach((n, score) => club.set(score, (club.get(score) ?? 0) + n));
  const clubTotal = [...club.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const seen = [...you.keys(), ...[...club.entries()].filter(([, n]) => n > 0).map(([s]) => s)];
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'score', label: 'Score', type: 'int' },
    { key: 'you_pct', label: 'You %', type: 'number' },
    { key: 'club_pct', label: 'Club %', type: 'number' },
  ];
  const lo = seen.length === 0 ? 51 : Math.min(...seen);
  const rows = Array.from({ length: 51 - lo }, (_, i) => {
    const score = lo + i;
    return {
      score,
      you_pct: share(you.get(score) ?? 0, rounds.length),
      club_pct: share(club.get(score) ?? 0, clubTotal),
    };
  });
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'score', y: ['you_pct', 'club_pct'] }) };
}

export function learningCurveModel(curve: ShooterInsights['learning_curve']): ChartModel {
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'k', label: 'Career event #', type: 'int' },
    { key: 'you', label: 'You (adjusted)', type: 'number' },
    { key: 'club', label: 'Club median', type: 'number' },
  ];
  const rows = curve.map((p) => ({ k: p.k, you: p.value, club: p.club_median }));
  return { columns, rows, option: lineOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'k', y: ['you', 'club'] }) };
}

export function splitsModel(splits: SplitRow[]): ChartModel {
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'key', label: 'Group', type: 'string' },
    { key: 'n_rounds', label: 'Rounds', type: 'int' },
    { key: 'avg', label: 'Average', type: 'number' },
    { key: 'median', label: 'Median', type: 'number' },
    { key: 'best', label: 'Best', type: 'int' },
  ];
  const rows = splits.map((s) => ({ key: s.key, n_rounds: s.n_rounds, avg: round1(s.avg), median: s.median, best: s.best }));
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'key', y: ['avg'] }) };
}

export function activeYears(rounds: ShooterRound[]): number[] {
  return [...new Set(rounds.map((r) => Number(r.event_date.slice(0, 4))))].sort((a, b) => b - a);
}

/** Best score per attended date in `year`, for the calendar heatmap. */
export function attendanceModel(rounds: ShooterRound[], year: number): ChartModel {
  const best = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of rounds) {
    if (!r.event_date.startsWith(`${year}-`)) continue;
    best.set(r.event_date, Math.max(best.get(r.event_date) ?? 0, r.score));
  }
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'date', label: 'Date', type: 'date' },
    { key: 'score', label: 'Best score', type: 'int' },
  ];
  const rows = [...best.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([date, score]) => ({ date, score }));
  return { columns, rows, option: calendarOption({ columns, rows }, { date: 'date', value: 'score', year }) };
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/QueryChart.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { UseQueryResult } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';

/** Loading / error / empty states for one chart; renders `children(data)` (a ChartFrame) once data is usable. */
export function QueryChart<T>({
  title,
  query,
  isEmpty,
  emptyText,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  query: UseQueryResult<T>;
  isEmpty: (data: T) => boolean;
  emptyText: string;
  children: (data: T) => ReactNode;
}) {
  if (query.isPending) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <Skeleton className="h-64" />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  if (query.isError) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <EmptyState title={`Couldn't load ${title.toLowerCase()}`} description={query.error.message} />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  if (isEmpty(query.data)) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <EmptyState title={emptyText} />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  return <>{children(query.data)}</>;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/PbTable.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { PersonalBest } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

/** Overall first, then calendar years newest first (Plan 06 PbOut scopes 'overall' and 'year'). */
function pbOrder(a: PersonalBest, b: PersonalBest): number {
  if (a.scope !== b.scope) return a.scope === 'overall' ? -1 : 1;
  return b.key.localeCompare(a.key);
}

export function PbTable({ pbs }: { pbs: PersonalBest[] }) {
  const sorted = [...pbs].sort(pbOrder);
  return (
    <Card title="Personal bests">
      {sorted.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">No personal bests yet</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table aria-label="Personal bests" className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="text-left text-text-muted">
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2">Best of</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="py-2 pr-2 text-right">Score</th>
                  <th scope="col" className="py-2">Date</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {sorted.map((pb) => (
                  <tr key={`${pb.scope}-${pb.key}`} className="border-t border-outline-variant">
                    <td className="py-2 pr-2">{pb.scope === 'overall' ? 'Overall' : pb.key}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 pr-2 text-right tabular-nums">{pb.score}</td>
                    <td className="py-2">
                      <Link to={`/events/${pb.event_date}`}>{formatDay(pb.event_date)}</Link>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">
            Personal bests by season, month, round type, gauge and weather are the Best column of the Splits chart’s
            table.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

Add `import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';` to the imports of `frontend/src/features/shooters/api.ts`
(Task 2 already imports `useQuery`, `api` and `unwrap`) and append the four hooks (Decision D25 — the Step 12 test is
their first failing test):
```ts
export function useShooterRounds(id: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}/rounds', id, { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}/rounds', { params: { path: { id }, query: { round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}

export function useShooterRating(id: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}/rating', id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}/rating', { params: { path: { id } } })),
  });
}

export function useShooterSplits(id: number, by: SplitBy) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}/splits', id, { by, round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}/splits', { params: { path: { id }, query: { by, round_type: roundTypes } } }),
      ),
  });
}

/** Club score distribution per year; this feature sums the years for "distribution vs club" (Decision D11). */
export function useClubDistribution() {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/club/distribution', { by: 'year', round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/distribution', { params: { query: { by: 'year', round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/shooters/components/ProfileCharts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { Chip } from '../../../components/ui/Chip';
import { enumCodec, intCodec, useUrlState } from '../../../lib/useUrlState';
import {
  useClubDistribution,
  useShooterInsights,
  useShooterRating,
  useShooterRounds,
  useShooterSplits,
} from '../api';
import type { SplitBy } from '../api';
import {
  activeYears,
  attendanceModel,
  distributionModel,
  learningCurveModel,
  ratingModel,
  splitsModel,
  trendModel,
} from '../charts';
import { QueryChart } from './QueryChart';

const SPLIT_LABELS: Record<SplitBy, string> = {
  year: 'Year',
  season: 'Season',
  month: 'Month',
  round_type: 'Round type',
  gauge: 'Gauge',
  temp_band: 'Temperature',
  wind_band: 'Wind',
  precip_band: 'Rain',
};
const SPLITS = Object.keys(SPLIT_LABELS) as SplitBy[];
const splitCodec = enumCodec(SPLITS); // an unknown ?split= reads as the default 'year'

function RatingChart({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useShooterRating(shooterId);
  return (
    <QueryChart title="Rating" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.points.length === 0} emptyText="No rating yet">
      {(rating) => {
        const m = ratingModel(rating.points);
        return (
          <ChartFrame title="Rating" subtitle="Skill estimate with its 95% band; the pin marks the peak" option={m.option}
            columns={m.columns} rows={m.rows} csvName={`rating-${shooterId}`} ariaLabel="Rating over time with uncertainty band" urlKey="rating" />
        );
      }}
    </QueryChart>
  );
}

function TrendChart({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useShooterRounds(shooterId);
  return (
    <QueryChart title="Scores over time" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText="No rounds yet">
      {(rounds) => {
        const m = trendModel(rounds);
        return (
          <ChartFrame title="Scores over time" subtitle="Raw and field-adjusted scores; pins mark personal bests" option={m.option}
            columns={m.columns} rows={m.rows} csvName={`scores-${shooterId}`} ariaLabel="Scores and adjusted scores per round" urlKey="trend" />
        );
      }}
    </QueryChart>
  );
}

function DistributionChart({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const rounds = useShooterRounds(shooterId);
  const club = useClubDistribution();
  const title = 'Score distribution vs club';
  return (
    <QueryChart title={title} query={rounds} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText="No rounds yet">
      {(mine) => (
        <QueryChart title={title} query={club} isEmpty={() => false} emptyText="">
          {(groups) => {
            const m = distributionModel(mine, groups);
            return (
              <ChartFrame title={title} subtitle="Share of rounds at each score" option={m.option} columns={m.columns}
                rows={m.rows} csvName={`distribution-${shooterId}`} ariaLabel="Score distribution compared with the club" urlKey="dist" />
            );
          }}
        </QueryChart>
      )}
    </QueryChart>
  );
}

function LearningCurveChart({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useShooterInsights(shooterId);
  return (
    <QueryChart title="Learning curve vs club" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.learning_curve.length === 0}
      emptyText="Not enough events for a learning curve">
      {(insights) => {
        const m = learningCurveModel(insights.learning_curve);
        return (
          <ChartFrame title="Learning curve vs club" subtitle="Adjusted score by career event number" option={m.option}
            columns={m.columns} rows={m.rows} csvName={`learning-${shooterId}`} ariaLabel="Learning curve compared with the club" urlKey="learn" />
        );
      }}
    </QueryChart>
  );
}

function SplitsChart({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const [by, setBy] = useUrlState('split', splitCodec, 'year');
  const query = useShooterSplits(shooterId, by);
  const label = SPLIT_LABELS[by];
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div role="group" aria-label="Split by" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {SPLITS.map((s) => (
          <Chip key={s} selected={s === by} onClick={() => setBy(s)}>
            {SPLIT_LABELS[s]}
          </Chip>
        ))}
      </div>
      <QueryChart title="Splits" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText="No rounds in this split">
        {(splits) => {
          const m = splitsModel(splits);
          return (
            <ChartFrame title={`Splits by ${label}`} subtitle="Average score per group; best and round counts in the table"
              option={m.option} columns={m.columns} rows={m.rows} csvName={`splits-${by}-${shooterId}`}
              ariaLabel={`Average score by ${label.toLowerCase()}`} urlKey="splits" />
          );
        }}
      </QueryChart>
    </div>
  );
}

function AttendanceCalendar({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useShooterRounds(shooterId);
  // `calYear`, not `cal`: the ChartFrame below keeps its Table/Fullscreen view state under urlKey "cal" (C10).
  const [yearParam, setYear] = useUrlState('calYear', intCodec, 0);
  return (
    <QueryChart title="Attendance calendar" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText="No rounds yet">
      {(rounds) => {
        const years = activeYears(rounds);
        const year = years.includes(yearParam) ? yearParam : (years[0] as number);
        const m = attendanceModel(rounds, year);
        const yearChips = (
          <div role="group" aria-label="Calendar year" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {years.map((y) => (
              <Chip key={y} selected={y === year} onClick={() => setYear(y)}>
                {String(y)}
              </Chip>
            ))}
          </div>
        );
        return (
          <ChartFrame title={`Attendance calendar ${year}`} subtitle="Best score on each Sunday attended" option={m.option}
            columns={m.columns} rows={m.rows} csvName={`attendance-${year}-${shooterId}`}
            ariaLabel={`Attendance calendar for ${year}`} urlKey="cal" actions={yearChips} />
        );
      }}
    </QueryChart>
  );
}

export function ProfileCharts({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
      <RatingChart shooterId={shooterId} />
      <TrendChart shooterId={shooterId} />
      <DistributionChart shooterId={shooterId} />
      <LearningCurveChart shooterId={shooterId} />
      <SplitsChart shooterId={shooterId} />
      <AttendanceCalendar shooterId={shooterId} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 4: Club dashboard (master Plan 08 T3)

**Branch:** `task/08-3-club` · **Depends on:** Plan 06 T7 (`/api/club/summary`, `attendance`, `cohorts`,
`distribution`), Plan 06 T9 (`regulars`, `conversion`, `parity`, `trends`), Plan 07 T1, T2, T4–T8 (UI kit, layout +
round-type filter, client, builders, `ChartFrame`, `ChartCard`, `/api/explore`); Plan 04 T2 (e2e seed).

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/format.ts`, `format.test.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/api.ts` (types in Step 7; hooks in Steps 14 and 18)
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/mocks.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/charts.ts`, `charts.test.ts`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/components/ChartSlot.tsx`, `ChartSlot.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/components/RegularsCard.tsx`, `RegularsCard.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/components/ClubCharts.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/components/SummaryStats.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/components/TurnoutWeatherCard.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/pages/ClubPage.tsx`, `ClubPage.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/club/routes.tsx`, `routes.test.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/e2e/club.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 07, verbatim from Plan 07's code):
  - `src/api/client.ts`: `api` (openapi-fetch `createClient<paths>`) and
    `unwrap<T>(request: Promise<FetchResult<T>>): Promise<T>` (any non-2xx throws `ApiError`, whose `message` is the
    server's `error.message`); `paths` from the generated `src/api/schema.d.ts` (`import type`).
  - `src/lib/roundTypes.ts`: `useRoundTypes(): [RoundTypeValue[], (next: RoundTypeValue[]) => void]` — the global
    round-type filter in the `rt` query parameter (`[]` = no filter). A hook calls `const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();`,
    puts `roundTypes` in its query key and sends it as `round_type` (C10).
  - `src/app/registry.ts`: `interface NavItem { label: string; path: string; icon: LucideIcon; order: number; mobileTab?: boolean; adminOnly?: boolean }`.
  - `src/components/ui/`: `Card({ title?: ReactNode; subtitle?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; className?: string; children?: ReactNode })`
    (a `<section>` labelled by its title, role `region`);
    `Stat({ label: string; value: ReactNode; delta?: number | null; formatDelta?: (magnitude: number) => string; hint?: string })`;
    `EmptyState({ title: string; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode })`;
    `Skeleton({ label?: string; lines?: number; className?: string })` (role `status`).
  - `src/components/charts/types.ts`: `interface TabularData { columns: { key: string; label: string; type: 'date' | 'number' | 'string' | 'int' }[]; rows: Record<string, string | number | null>[] }`.
  - `src/components/charts/ChartFrame.tsx`: `ChartFrame({ title: string; subtitle?: string; option: EChartsOption; columns: TabularData['columns']; rows: TabularData['rows']; csvName: string; ariaLabel: string; urlKey: string; zoom?: 'x' | 'y' | 'xy' | 'none'; onEvents?; actions?: ReactNode; controls?: ReactNode; height?: number; rowHref?: (row: TabularRow) => string })`
    — a `Card` titled `title` whose header has buttons named exactly `Table`, `CSV` and `Fullscreen`; its view state lives
    in the `urlKey` query parameter, so every frame on the page needs its own `urlKey`.
  - `src/components/charts/ChartCard.tsx`: `ChartCard({ title: string; subtitle?: string; spec: QuerySpec; chartTypes: ChartType[]; allowedGroupBy?: Dim[]; allowedMetrics?: Metric[]; urlKey: string; drill?: (row: TabularRow) => string })`
    (a `ChartFrame` plus metric/group-by chips over `POST /api/explore`; it merges the global `rt` filter into
    `spec.filters.round_types` itself); `src/components/charts/explore.ts`: `querySpec(input: QuerySpecInput): QuerySpec`
    with `type QuerySpecInput = Pick<QuerySpec, 'metric'> & Partial<Omit<QuerySpec, 'metric' | 'filters'>> & { filters?: Partial<Filters> }`
    — it fills `agg`, `group_by`, `sort`, `limit` and every `filters` field with the server defaults, because the
    generated `QuerySpec` type marks those fields required.
  - Builders (pure, each returns an `EChartsOption`):
    `lineOption(data: TabularData, opts: { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; band?: { lower: string; upper: string; name?: string }; markers?: { flag: string; name: string }; area?: boolean; smooth?: boolean; yName?: string; scaleY?: boolean })`;
    `barOption(data: TabularData, opts: { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; horizontal?: boolean; stack?: boolean; yName?: string; labels?: boolean })`;
    `ridgelineOption(data: TabularData, opts: { group: string; value: string; min?: number; max?: number; step?: number; bandwidth?: number; overlap?: number })`
    — **one row per observation**: one ridge per distinct `group` (first group at the top), a Gaussian kernel density of
    `value` over 0..50.
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, options?: { route?: string; path?: string; queryClient?: QueryClient; role?: 'viewer' | 'admin' | null | 'unset' }): ProvidersResult`
    (RTL result plus `user`, `queryClient`, `router`; queries never retry); `src/test/msw/server.ts`: `server`. e2e:
    `test`, `expect` from `e2e/fixtures.ts` (viewer storage state by default).
- Consumes (Plan 06 response models as written):
  - `GET /api/club/summary?round_type=` → `ClubSummaryOut = { first_event: string | null; last_event: string | null; n_events: number; n_scored_events: number; n_held_events: number; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; avg_score: number | null; median_score: number | null; top_score: number | null; n_perfect: number; clays_thrown: number; clays_broken: number; avg_head_count: number | null; shooters_by_status: Record<string, number>; status_by_year: { year: number; member_rounds: number; guest_rounds: number; deceased_rounds: number; unrecorded_rounds: number }[] }`
    (`first_event`/`last_event` = the first/last *scored* event; `status_by_year` counts rounds by each round's recorded
    `status`, C7).
  - `GET /api/club/attendance` → `AttendanceOut[]`, `AttendanceOut = { event_date: string; head_count: number | null; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; has_scores: boolean; results_complete: boolean }`
    (every event since 2018-12-30; `n_rounds` > `n_shooters` on multi-round days).
  - `GET /api/club/cohorts` → `CohortOut[]`, `CohortOut = { year: number; n_new: number; n_returned: number; retention: { offset: number; n_active: number; share: number }[] }`
    (`left_censored` shooters excluded server-side; `offset` 0 is the cohort year itself (share 1), offset k = the
    share still shooting k calendar years later).
  - `GET /api/club/distribution?by=year&round_type=` → `DistributionOut[]`, `DistributionOut = { key: string; n: number; mean: number; median: number; p10: number; p25: number; p75: number; p90: number; counts: number[] }`
    (`counts[s]` = rounds scoring `s`, length 51).
  - `GET /api/club/regulars` → `RegularsOut = { as_of: string; n_held_window: number; core: { shooter_id: number; display_name: string; events_attended: number; share: number }[]; lapsed: { shooter_id: number; display_name: string; last_event: string }[] }`.
  - `GET /api/club/conversion` → `ConversionYearOut[]`, `ConversionYearOut = { year: number; new_guests: number; converted: number; median_days_to_convert: number | null }`
    (new guests by the year of their first guest round; conversions by the year of the first member round after it).
  - `GET /api/club/parity?by=year` → `ParityYearOut[]`, `ParityYearOut = { year: number; n_events: number; distinct_winners: number; top3_share: number | null; favorite_win_rate: number | null }` (fractions 0–1).
  - `GET /api/club/trends` → `ClubTrendsOut = { as_of: string; years: YearTrendOut[]; events: EventTrendOut[]; months: MonthTrendOut[] }` with
    `YearTrendOut = { year: number; events_held: number; mean_head_count: number | null; unique_shooters: number; ytd_events: number; ytd_rounds: number; ytd_unique_shooters: number; ytd_events_yoy: number | null }`
    (`ytd_events_yoy` = fractional change of year-to-date held events vs the previous year),
    `EventTrendOut = { event_date: string; top_score: number; median: number; difficulty: number | null; top_score_rolling8: number; median_rolling8: number; difficulty_rolling8: number | null }`
    (held events only; the `*_rolling8` columns are the server's means over the last 8 held events, min 1 — this task
    never computes a rolling mean) and
    `MonthTrendOut = { month: number; n_events: number; mean_head_count: number | null; mean_median: number | null }`
    (always the twelve months; a month with no held event has `n_events` 0).
  - `POST /api/explore` (through `ChartCard`, Plan 07 T7), spec `querySpec({ metric: 'attendance', agg: 'avg', group_by: ['condition'] })`.
- MSW rule: `club/mocks.ts` owns the `*/api/club/*` default handlers. They are branch-free (a fixed response), because
  `mocks.ts` counts toward coverage, and `routes.test.tsx` runs on them on purpose so every handler line executes; every
  other test pins what it reads with `server.use(...)`. `POST /api/explore` belongs to the explorer feature (Plan 07 T8),
  so every test that renders the turnout card pins it.
- Produces: route `/club`; nav `{ label: 'Club', path: '/club', icon: Landmark, order: 50 }`. Nothing outside
  `features/club/` imports this feature's files.

Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 run concurrently in separate worktrees and every stack publishes host port 8080, so this task
builds its own image tag, runs Compose as its own project and holds the machine-wide lock directory
`/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime. The
`EXIT` trap tears the stack down and releases the lock even though this run fails. A lock with no
`docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is left over from a crashed run: `rmdir` it. Run from the
worktree root (`rm -rf secrets` deletes only this worktree's generated, gitignored dev secrets so the known e2e

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `format.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/club/format.ts`:
```ts
const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });
const MONTH = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' });

/** '2018-12-30' → 'Dec 30, 2018' (UTC in, UTC out: the day never shifts). */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

export function monthShort(month: number): string {
  return MONTH.format(new Date(Date.UTC(2000, month - 1, 1)));
}

export function round1(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
}

/** Fraction 0–1 → percent with one decimal; null stays null. */
export function pct1(fraction: number | null): number | null {
  return fraction === null ? null : round1(100 * fraction);
}
```

`api.ts` starts as types only (types emit no JavaScript); each TanStack hook is added in the GREEN step of the first

Create `frontend/src/features/club/api.ts`:
```ts
import type { paths } from '../../api/schema';

type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } } ? R : never;

export type ClubSummary = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/summary']['get']>;
export type StatusYear = ClubSummary['status_by_year'][number];
export type AttendancePoint = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/attendance']['get']>[number];
export type Cohort = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/cohorts']['get']>[number];
export type DistributionGroup = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/distribution']['get']>[number];
export type Regulars = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/regulars']['get']>;
export type CoreRegular = Regulars['core'][number];
export type LapsedRegular = Regulars['lapsed'][number];
export type ConversionYear = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/conversion']['get']>[number];
export type ParityYear = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/parity']['get']>[number];
export type ClubTrends = JsonOf<paths['/api/club/trends']['get']>;
export type YearTrend = ClubTrends['years'][number];
export type EventTrend = ClubTrends['events'][number];
export type MonthTrend = ClubTrends['months'][number];
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/mocks.ts`. Every Plan 06 required field is set. The numbers are the fixture's

  });
});

describe('yearTrendsModel', () => {
  it('rounds the mean head count and shows the year-to-date change as a percentage', () => {
    expect(yearTrendsModel(clubTrends.years).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2020', events_held: 39, mean_head_count: 20.7, unique_shooters: 92, ytd_events: 26, ytd_events_yoy_pct: null },
      { year: '2025', events_held: 48, mean_head_count: 25.9, unique_shooters: 139, ytd_events: 35, ytd_events_yoy_pct: 2.9 },
      { year: '2026', events_held: 36, mean_head_count: 26.9, unique_shooters: 129, ytd_events: 36, ytd_events_yoy_pct: 2.9 },
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps a year without head counts empty', () => {

});

describe('seasonalityModel', () => {

    expect(seasonalityModel([...clubTrends.months].reverse()).rows).toEqual([

      { month: 'Sep', n_events: 27, mean_head_count: 22, mean_median: 35.9 },
      { month: 'Dec', n_events: 24, mean_head_count: 21.4, mean_median: 35.5 },
    ]);

  it('keeps a month without held events empty', () => {
    expect(seasonalityModel([{ month: 2, n_events: 0, mean_head_count: null, mean_median: null }]).rows).toEqual([
      { month: 'Feb', n_events: 0, mean_head_count: null, mean_median: null },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('score trend and difficulty', () => {
  it("uses the server's 8-event rolling means, rounded to one decimal, in date order", () => {
    expect(scoreTrendModel([...clubTrends.events].reverse()).rows).toEqual([
      { event_date: '2026-09-06', median: 36, median_rolling8: 38.1, top_score: 48, top_score_rolling8: 46.9 },
      { event_date: '2026-09-13', median: 34, median_rolling8: 37.4, top_score: 42, top_score_rolling8: 46.5 },
      { event_date: '2026-09-27', median: 39, median_rolling8: 37.3, top_score: 49, top_score_rolling8: 46.6 },
    ]);
  });

  it('shows difficulty and its rolling mean, keeping events without a difficulty empty', () => {
    const unrated: EventTrend = { event_date: '2020-01-05', top_score: 45, median: 34, difficulty: null, top_score_rolling8: 45, median_rolling8: 34, difficulty_rolling8: null };
    expect(difficultyModel([...clubTrends.events, unrated]).rows).toEqual([
      { event_date: '2020-01-05', difficulty: null, difficulty_rolling8: null },
      { event_date: '2026-09-06', difficulty: 0.3, difficulty_rolling8: 0.4 },
      { event_date: '2026-09-13', difficulty: 2.1, difficulty_rolling8: 0.6 },
      { event_date: '2026-09-27', difficulty: -1.4, difficulty_rolling8: 0.3 },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('distribution', () => {
  it('expands the counts into one row per round, newest year first, for the ridgeline', () => {
    expect(distributionObservations(clubDistribution).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2026', score: 41 },
      { year: '2026', score: 50 },
      { year: '2025', score: 40 },
      { year: '2025', score: 40 },
      { year: '2025', score: 41 },
      { year: '2025', score: 42 },
    ]);

  it('tables the rounds, mean and quantiles per year', () => {
    expect(distributionModel(clubDistribution).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2025', n: 4, mean: 40.8, p10: 40, p25: 40, median: 40.5, p75: 41.25, p90: 41.7 },
      { year: '2026', n: 2, mean: 45.5, p10: 41.9, p25: 43.25, median: 45.5, p75: 47.75, p90: 49.1 },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('cohort models', () => {
  const rows = [
    { year: '2024', n_new: 35, n_returned: 18, retained_1y_pct: 40, retained_2y_pct: 25.7 },
    { year: '2025', n_new: 57, n_returned: 24, retained_1y_pct: 35.1, retained_2y_pct: null },
    { year: '2026', n_new: 41, n_returned: 15, retained_1y_pct: null, retained_2y_pct: null },
  ];

  it('newcomers per year with 1- and 2-year retention read from the retention offsets', () => {
    expect(newcomersModel([...clubCohorts].reverse()).rows).toEqual(rows);
  });

  it('retention charts the same per-year rows', () => {
    expect(retentionModel(clubCohorts).rows).toEqual(rows);
  });
});

describe('membership, conversion and parity', () => {
  it('splits rounds by the status recorded on each round', () => {
    expect(memberGuestModel(clubSummary.status_by_year).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2025', member_rounds: 1210, guest_rounds: 45, deceased_rounds: 12, unrecorded_rounds: 0 },
      { year: '2026', member_rounds: 915, guest_rounds: 54, deceased_rounds: 0, unrecorded_rounds: 0 },
    ]);
  });

  it('computes the conversion rate from new guests and conversions', () => {
    expect(conversionModel(clubConversion).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2024', new_guests: 11, converted: 0, rate_pct: 0, median_days: null },
      { year: '2025', new_guests: 31, converted: 2, rate_pct: 6.5, median_days: 80.5 },
      { year: '2026', new_guests: 29, converted: 4, rate_pct: 13.8, median_days: 714 },
    ]);
  });

  it('has no conversion rate for a year without new guests', () => {
    const year: ConversionYear = { year: 2027, new_guests: 0, converted: 1, median_days_to_convert: 30 };
    expect(conversionModel([year]).rows[0]?.rate_pct).toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows parity shares as percentages and keeps missing shares empty', () => {
    const unrated: ParityYear = { year: 2020, n_events: 39, distinct_winners: 14, top3_share: null, favorite_win_rate: null };
    expect(parityModel([unrated, ...clubParity]).rows).toEqual([
      { year: '2020', n_events: 39, distinct_winners: 14, top3_share_pct: null, favorite_win_pct: null },
      { year: '2025', n_events: 48, distinct_winners: 23, top3_share_pct: 39, favorite_win_pct: 14.6 },
      { year: '2026', n_events: 36, distinct_winners: 18, top3_share_pct: 38.6, favorite_win_pct: 11.1 },
    ]);
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement `charts.ts`**

Create `frontend/src/features/club/charts.ts`:
```ts
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import { barOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/bar';
import { lineOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/line';
import { ridgelineOption } from '../../components/charts/builders/ridgeline';
import type { TabularData } from '../../components/charts/types';
import type {
  AttendancePoint,
  Cohort,
  ConversionYear,
  DistributionGroup,
  EventTrend,
  MonthTrend,
  ParityYear,
  StatusYear,
  YearTrend,
} from './api';
import { monthShort, pct1, round1 } from './format';

export type ChartModel = TabularData & { option: EChartsOption };

type Column = TabularData['columns'][number];
const col = (key: string, label: string, type: Column['type']): Column => ({ key, label, type });
const r1 = (v: number | null) => (v === null ? null : round1(v));

const byDate = <T extends { event_date: string }>(rows: T[]) =>
  [...rows].sort((a, b) => a.event_date.localeCompare(b.event_date));
const byYear = <T extends { year: number }>(rows: T[]) => [...rows].sort((a, b) => a.year - b.year);

/** Spec §4 "count vs rows": head count against score rows (more rows than shooters on multi-round days). */
export function attendanceModel(points: AttendancePoint[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('event_date', 'Date', 'date'),
    col('head_count', 'Head count', 'int'),
    col('rounds', 'Score rows', 'int'),
    col('shooters', 'Shooters with scores', 'int'),
  ];
  const rows = byDate(points).map((p) => ({
    event_date: p.event_date,
    head_count: p.head_count,
    rounds: p.n_rounds,
    shooters: p.n_shooters,
  }));
  return { columns, rows, option: lineOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'event_date', y: ['head_count', 'rounds'] }) };
}

export function yearTrendsModel(years: YearTrend[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
    col('events_held', 'Events held', 'int'),
    col('mean_head_count', 'Mean head count', 'number'),
    col('unique_shooters', 'Unique shooters', 'int'),
    col('ytd_events', 'Events held year to date', 'int'),
    col('ytd_events_yoy_pct', 'Year-to-date change %', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = byYear(years).map((y) => ({
    year: String(y.year),
    events_held: y.events_held,
    mean_head_count: r1(y.mean_head_count),
    unique_shooters: y.unique_shooters,
    ytd_events: y.ytd_events,
    ytd_events_yoy_pct: pct1(y.ytd_events_yoy),
  }));
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['unique_shooters', 'events_held'] }) };
}

export function seasonalityModel(months: MonthTrend[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('month', 'Month', 'string'),
    col('n_events', 'Events', 'int'),
    col('mean_head_count', 'Mean head count', 'number'),
    col('mean_median', 'Mean median score', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = [...months]
    .sort((a, b) => a.month - b.month)
    .map((m) => ({
      month: monthShort(m.month),
      n_events: m.n_events,
      mean_head_count: r1(m.mean_head_count),
      mean_median: r1(m.mean_median),
    }));
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'month', y: ['mean_head_count'] }) };
}

/** Median and top score per held event with the server's 8-event rolling means (Plan 06 T9). */
export function scoreTrendModel(events: EventTrend[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('event_date', 'Date', 'date'),
    col('median', 'Median', 'number'),
    col('median_rolling8', 'Median (8-event mean)', 'number'),
    col('top_score', 'Top score', 'int'),
    col('top_score_rolling8', 'Top score (8-event mean)', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = byDate(events).map((e) => ({
    event_date: e.event_date,
    median: e.median,
    median_rolling8: round1(e.median_rolling8),
    top_score: e.top_score,
    top_score_rolling8: round1(e.top_score_rolling8),
  }));
  return {
    columns,
    rows,
    option: lineOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'event_date', y: ['median', 'median_rolling8', 'top_score', 'top_score_rolling8'] }),
  };
}

/** Published difficulty (positive = harder than a typical recent day, C7) with the server's rolling mean. */
export function difficultyModel(events: EventTrend[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('event_date', 'Date', 'date'),
    col('difficulty', 'Difficulty', 'number'),
    col('difficulty_rolling8', 'Difficulty (8-event mean)', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = byDate(events).map((e) => ({
    event_date: e.event_date,
    difficulty: r1(e.difficulty),
    difficulty_rolling8: r1(e.difficulty_rolling8),
  }));
  return { columns, rows, option: lineOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'event_date', y: ['difficulty', 'difficulty_rolling8'] }) };
}

/** One (year, score) row per round — the ridgeline builder's input — newest year first, so it is the top ridge. */
export function distributionObservations(groups: DistributionGroup[]): TabularData {
  const newestFirst = [...groups].sort((a, b) => b.key.localeCompare(a.key));
  return {
    columns: [col('year', 'Year', 'string'), col('score', 'Score', 'int')],
    rows: newestFirst.flatMap((g) =>
      g.counts.flatMap((n, score) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => ({ year: g.key, score }))),
    ),
  };
}

export function distributionModel(groups: DistributionGroup[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
    col('n', 'Rounds', 'int'),
    col('mean', 'Mean', 'number'),
    col('p10', 'P10', 'number'),
    col('p25', 'P25', 'number'),
    col('median', 'Median', 'number'),
    col('p75', 'P75', 'number'),
    col('p90', 'P90', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = [...groups]
    .sort((a, b) => a.key.localeCompare(b.key))
    .map((g) => ({ year: g.key, n: g.n, mean: round1(g.mean), p10: g.p10, p25: g.p25, median: g.median, p75: g.p75, p90: g.p90 }));
  return { columns, rows, option: ridgelineOption(distributionObservations(groups), { group: 'year', value: 'score' }) };
}

const cohortColumns = [
  col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
  col('n_new', 'Newcomers', 'int'),
  col('n_returned', 'Came back', 'int'),
  col('retained_1y_pct', '1-year retention %', 'number'),
  col('retained_2y_pct', '2-year retention %', 'number'),
];

/** Share of a cohort still shooting `offset` calendar years later; null until that year has data. */
function retentionPct(c: Cohort, offset: number): number | null {
  return pct1(c.retention.find((r) => r.offset === offset)?.share ?? null);
}

function cohortRows(cohorts: Cohort[]) {
  return byYear(cohorts).map((c) => ({
    year: String(c.year),
    n_new: c.n_new,
    n_returned: c.n_returned,
    retained_1y_pct: retentionPct(c, 1),
    retained_2y_pct: retentionPct(c, 2),
  }));
}

export function newcomersModel(cohorts: Cohort[]): ChartModel {
  const rows = cohortRows(cohorts);
  return { columns: cohortColumns, rows, option: barOption({ columns: cohortColumns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['n_new', 'n_returned'] }) };
}

export function retentionModel(cohorts: Cohort[]): ChartModel {
  const rows = cohortRows(cohorts);
  return {
    columns: cohortColumns,
    rows,
    option: lineOption({ columns: cohortColumns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['retained_1y_pct', 'retained_2y_pct'] }),
  };
}

/** Rounds per year by the status recorded on each round (C7), stacked. */
export function memberGuestModel(statusByYear: StatusYear[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
    col('member_rounds', 'Member rounds', 'int'),
    col('guest_rounds', 'Guest rounds', 'int'),
    col('deceased_rounds', 'In memoriam rounds', 'int'),
    col('unrecorded_rounds', 'Status not recorded', 'int'),
  ];
  const rows = byYear(statusByYear).map((y) => ({
    year: String(y.year),
    member_rounds: y.member_rounds,
    guest_rounds: y.guest_rounds,
    deceased_rounds: y.deceased_rounds,
    unrecorded_rounds: y.unrecorded_rounds,
  }));
  return {
    columns,
    rows,
    option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['member_rounds', 'guest_rounds', 'deceased_rounds', 'unrecorded_rounds'], stack: true }),
  };
}

/** Rate = conversions that year / new guests that year; null when the year had no new guests. */
export function conversionModel(years: ConversionYear[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
    col('new_guests', 'First-time guests', 'int'),
    col('converted', 'Became members', 'int'),
    col('rate_pct', 'Conversion %', 'number'),
    col('median_days', 'Median days to join', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = byYear(years).map((y) => ({
    year: String(y.year),
    new_guests: y.new_guests,
    converted: y.converted,
    rate_pct: y.new_guests === 0 ? null : pct1(y.converted / y.new_guests),
    median_days: y.median_days_to_convert,
  }));
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['new_guests', 'converted'] }) };
}

export function parityModel(years: ParityYear[]): ChartModel {
  const columns = [
    col('year', 'Year', 'string'),
    col('n_events', 'Events', 'int'),
    col('distinct_winners', 'Different winners', 'int'),
    col('top3_share_pct', 'Top-3 share of wins %', 'number'),
    col('favorite_win_pct', 'Favorite win rate %', 'number'),
  ];
  const rows = byYear(years).map((y) => ({
    year: String(y.year),
    n_events: y.n_events,
    distinct_winners: y.distinct_winners,
    top3_share_pct: pct1(y.top3_share),
    favorite_win_pct: pct1(y.favorite_win_rate),
  }));
  return { columns, rows, option: lineOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'year', y: ['top3_share_pct', 'favorite_win_pct'] }) };
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/components/ChartSlot.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { UseQueryResult } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';

/** Loading / error / empty states for one dashboard chart; `children(data)` renders the ChartFrame. */
export function ChartSlot<T>({
  title,
  query,
  isEmpty,
  emptyText,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  query: UseQueryResult<T>;
  isEmpty: (data: T) => boolean;
  emptyText: string;
  children: (data: T) => ReactNode;
}) {
  if (query.isPending) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <Skeleton className="h-64" />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  if (query.isError) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <EmptyState title={`Couldn't load ${title.toLowerCase()}`} description={query.error.message} />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  if (isEmpty(query.data)) {
    return (
      <Card title={title}>
        <EmptyState title={emptyText} />
      </Card>
    );
  }
  return <>{children(query.data)}</>;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/components/RegularsCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { useClubRegulars } from '../api';
import type { CoreRegular, LapsedRegular } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

type Person = { shooter_id: number; display_name: string };

/** One titled list; core and lapsed regulars are different row types, so `detail` formats each one. */
function PeopleList<T extends Person>({
  title,
  hint,
  people,
  detail,
}: {
  title: string;
  hint: string;
  people: T[];
  detail: (person: T) => string;
}) {
  return (
    <section aria-label={title} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <h3 className="font-medium">{`${title} (${people.length})`}</h3>
      <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">{hint}</p>
      {people.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">None right now</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {people.map((p) => (
            <li key={p.shooter_id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-x-2">
              <Link to={`/shooters/${p.shooter_id}`}>{p.display_name}</Link>
              <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">{detail(p)}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export function RegularsCard() {
  const query = useClubRegulars();
  let body: ReactNode;
  if (query.isPending) body = <Skeleton className="h-40" />;
  else if (query.isError) body = <EmptyState title="Couldn't load regulars" description={query.error.message} />;
  else {
    const { n_held_window: heldWindow, core, lapsed } = query.data;
    body = (
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <PeopleList<CoreRegular>
          title="Core regulars"
          hint="At half or more of the held events in the last year"
          people={core}
          detail={(r) => `${r.events_attended} of ${heldWindow} held events`}
        />
        <PeopleList<LapsedRegular>
          title="Lapsed regulars"
          hint="Core regulars six months ago who have not shot in 90 days"
          people={lapsed}
          detail={(r) => `last out ${formatDay(r.last_event)}`}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
  return <Card title="Regulars">{body}</Card>;
}
```
