- [ ] **Step 18: Add the remaining hooks and implement the charts, cards and the page**

Add `import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';` to the imports of `frontend/src/features/club/api.ts` and
append these hooks (the Step 16 page test is their first failing test). Summary and distribution accept the global
round-type filter (C8), so they send it and key on it (C10); the other endpoints take no `round_type`:
```ts
export function useClubSummary() {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/club/summary', { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/summary', { params: { query: { round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}

export function useClubAttendance() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['/api/club/attendance'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/attendance')) });
}

export function useClubCohorts() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['/api/club/cohorts'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/cohorts')) });
}

/** Same key shape as features/shooters' useClubDistribution, so the TanStack cache is shared. */
export function useClubDistribution() {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/club/distribution', { by: 'year', round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/distribution', { params: { query: { by: 'year', round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}

export function useClubConversion() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['/api/club/conversion'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/conversion')) });
}

export function useClubParity() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/club/parity', { by: 'year' }],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/parity', { params: { query: { by: 'year' } } })),
  });
}

export function useClubTrends() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['/api/club/trends'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/club/trends')) });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/components/ClubCharts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import {
  useClubAttendance,
  useClubCohorts,
  useClubConversion,
  useClubDistribution,
  useClubParity,
  useClubSummary,
  useClubTrends,
} from '../api';
import {
  attendanceModel,
  conversionModel,
  difficultyModel,
  distributionModel,
  memberGuestModel,
  newcomersModel,
  parityModel,
  retentionModel,
  scoreTrendModel,
  seasonalityModel,
  yearTrendsModel,
} from '../charts';
import type { ChartModel } from '../charts';
import { ChartSlot } from './ChartSlot';

const NO_DATA = 'No data yet';

/** Every frame on this page has its own urlKey: ChartFrame keeps its Table/Fullscreen state under it (C10). */
function Frame({ title, subtitle, model, name, urlKey }: { title: string; subtitle: string; model: ChartModel; name: string; urlKey: string }) {
  return (
    <ChartFrame title={title} subtitle={subtitle} option={model.option} columns={model.columns} rows={model.rows}
      csvName={name} ariaLabel={`${title} chart`} urlKey={urlKey} />
  );
}

export function AttendanceChart() {
  const query = useClubAttendance();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Attendance" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText="No events yet">
      {(points) => {
        const since = points.reduce((min, p) => (p.event_date < min ? p.event_date : min), '9999').slice(0, 4);
        return <Frame title={`Attendance since ${since}`} subtitle="Head count vs score rows per event; shooters in the table"
          model={attendanceModel(points)} name="club-attendance" urlKey="att" />;
      }}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function YearTrendsChart() {
  const query = useClubTrends();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Shooters and events per year" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.years.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(trends) => <Frame title="Shooters and events per year" subtitle="Unique shooters and events held; year-to-date change in the table"
        model={yearTrendsModel(trends.years)} name="club-years" urlKey="years" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function SeasonalityChart() {
  const query = useClubTrends();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Seasonality" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.months.every((m) => m.n_events === 0)} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(trends) => <Frame title="Seasonality" subtitle="Average head count by month of the year (held events)"
        model={seasonalityModel(trends.months)} name="club-seasonality" urlKey="season" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function ScoreTrendChart() {
  const query = useClubTrends();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Median and top score" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.events.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(trends) => <Frame title="Median and top score" subtitle="Per held event, with 8-event rolling means"
        model={scoreTrendModel(trends.events)} name="club-scores" urlKey="scores" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function DifficultyChart() {
  const query = useClubTrends();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Difficulty by event" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.events.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(trends) => <Frame title="Difficulty by event" subtitle="Positive = harder than a typical recent day; 8-event rolling mean"
        model={difficultyModel(trends.events)} name="club-difficulty" urlKey="diff" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function DistributionChart() {
  const query = useClubDistribution();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Score distribution by year" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(groups) => <Frame title="Score distribution by year" subtitle="Density of round scores per year; quantiles in the table"
        model={distributionModel(groups)} name="club-distribution" urlKey="dist" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function NewcomersChart() {
  const query = useClubCohorts();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Newcomers per year" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(cohorts) => <Frame title="Newcomers per year" subtitle="First-time shooters and how many came back (earliest weeks excluded)"
        model={newcomersModel(cohorts)} name="club-newcomers" urlKey="new" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function RetentionChart() {
  const query = useClubCohorts();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Newcomer retention" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(cohorts) => <Frame title="Newcomer retention" subtitle="Share of each year's newcomers still shooting 1 and 2 years later"
        model={retentionModel(cohorts)} name="club-retention" urlKey="ret" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function MemberGuestChart() {
  const query = useClubSummary();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Rounds by member status" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.status_by_year.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(summary) => <Frame title="Rounds by member status" subtitle="Status as recorded on each round"
        model={memberGuestModel(summary.status_by_year)} name="club-status" urlKey="status" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function ConversionChart() {
  const query = useClubConversion();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Guest → member conversion" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(years) => <Frame title="Guest → member conversion" subtitle="First-time guests and guests who later shot as members; rate and median days in the table"
        model={conversionModel(years)} name="club-conversion" urlKey="conv" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}

export function ParityChart() {
  const query = useClubParity();
  return (
    <ChartSlot title="Parity" query={query} isEmpty={(d) => d.length === 0} emptyText={NO_DATA}>
      {(years) => <Frame title="Parity" subtitle="Top-3 share of wins and the favorite's win rate; different winners in the table"
        model={parityModel(years)} name="club-parity" urlKey="parity" />}
    </ChartSlot>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/components/SummaryStats.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import { useClubSummary } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

/** Header stats; on error they are omitted (the member-status chart shows the summary error). */
export function SummaryStats() {
  const query = useClubSummary();
  if (query.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-20" />;
  if (query.isError) return null;
  const s = query.data;
  const count = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString('en-US');
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5">
      <Stat label="Events held" value={count(s.n_held_events)} />
      <Stat label="Shooters" value={count(s.n_shooters)} />
      <Stat label="Rounds" value={count(s.n_rounds)} />
      <Stat label="Clays broken" value={count(s.clays_broken)} />
      <Stat label="First scores" value={s.first_event === null ? '—' : formatDay(s.first_event)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/club/components/TurnoutWeatherCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartCard } from '../../../components/charts/ChartCard';
import { querySpec } from '../../../components/charts/explore';

/**
 * Average head count per weather condition or band, through the Explorer (C9 metric `attendance`). `querySpec` fills
 * the server-defaulted fields the generated QuerySpec type marks required.
 */
const TURNOUT_SPEC = querySpec({ metric: 'attendance', agg: 'avg', group_by: ['condition'] });

export function TurnoutWeatherCard() {
  return (
    <ChartCard
      title="Turnout vs weather"
      subtitle="Average head count by weather condition or band"
      spec={TURNOUT_SPEC}
      chartTypes={['bar']}
      allowedGroupBy={['condition', 'temp_band', 'wind_band', 'precip_band']}
      allowedMetrics={['attendance']}
      urlKey="turnout"
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useId } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  AttendanceChart,
  ConversionChart,
  DifficultyChart,
  DistributionChart,
  MemberGuestChart,
  NewcomersChart,
  ParityChart,
  RetentionChart,
  ScoreTrendChart,
  SeasonalityChart,
  YearTrendsChart,
} from '../components/ClubCharts';
import { RegularsCard } from '../components/RegularsCard';
import { SummaryStats } from '../components/SummaryStats';
import { TurnoutWeatherCard } from '../components/TurnoutWeatherCard';

function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <h2 id={id} className="text-xl font-medium">{title}</h2>
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

export function ClubPage() {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-medium">Club</h1>
        <SummaryStats />
      </header>
      <Section title="Attendance">
        <AttendanceChart />
        <YearTrendsChart />
        <SeasonalityChart />
        <TurnoutWeatherCard />
      </Section>
      <Section title="Scores">
        <DistributionChart />
        <ScoreTrendChart />
        <DifficultyChart />
      </Section>
      <Section title="Membership">
        <NewcomersChart />
        <RetentionChart />
        <MemberGuestChart />
        <ConversionChart />
        <RegularsCard />
      </Section>
      <Section title="Competition">
        <ParityChart />
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Step 18 also switches `app/appRoutes.test.tsx` (Plan 07 T2/T3) from `getAllByRole('main')[0]` to a single
`getByRole('main')`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 07, verbatim from Plan 07's code):
  - `src/api/client.ts`: `api` (openapi-fetch `createClient<paths>`) and
    `unwrap<T>(request: Promise<FetchResult<T>>): Promise<T>` (any non-2xx throws `ApiError`);
    `src/api/errors.ts`: `class ApiError extends Error { readonly status: number; readonly code: string }` (`message` =
    the server's `error.message`); `paths` from the generated `src/api/schema.d.ts` (`import type`).
  - `src/lib/roundTypes.ts`: `useRoundTypes(): [RoundTypeValue[], (next: RoundTypeValue[]) => void]` — the global
    round-type filter in the `rt` query parameter (`[]` = no filter); hooks call `const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();`,
    key on it and send it as `round_type` (C10).
  - `src/lib/me.ts`: `getMe(): number | null`, `setMe(id: number): void`, `clearMe(): void` (localStorage, wrapped in
    try/catch).
  - `src/app/registry.ts`: `interface NavItem { label: string; path: string; icon: LucideIcon; order: number; mobileTab?: boolean; adminOnly?: boolean }`.
  - `src/components/ui/`: `Card({ title?: ReactNode; subtitle?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; className?: string; children?: ReactNode })`
    (role `region` named by its title);
    `Stat({ label: string; value: ReactNode; delta?: number | null; formatDelta?: (magnitude: number) => string; hint?: string })`;
    `EmptyState({ title: string; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode })`;
    `Skeleton({ label?: string; lines?: number; className?: string })`;
    `Button(ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: 'primary' | 'tonal' | 'ghost' | 'danger'; loading?: boolean; icon?: ReactNode })`
    (default `variant="primary"`, default `type="button"`).
  - `src/components/charts/ChartFrame.tsx`: `ChartFrame({ title: string; subtitle?: string; option: EChartsOption; columns: TabularData['columns']; rows: TabularData['rows']; csvName: string; ariaLabel: string; urlKey: string; zoom?: 'x' | 'y' | 'xy' | 'none'; onEvents?; actions?: ReactNode; controls?: ReactNode; height?: number; rowHref?: (row: TabularRow) => string })`
    (buttons named exactly `Table`, `CSV`, `Fullscreen`; view state in the `urlKey` query parameter);
    `src/components/charts/types.ts`: `interface TabularData { columns: { key: string; label: string; type: 'date' | 'number' | 'string' | 'int' }[]; rows: Record<string, string | number | null>[] }`;
    `src/components/charts/builders/bar.ts`: `barOption(data: TabularData, opts: { x: string; y: string[]; seriesBy?: string; horizontal?: boolean; stack?: boolean; yName?: string; labels?: boolean }): EChartsOption`.
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, options?: { route?: string; path?: string; queryClient?: QueryClient; role?: 'viewer' | 'admin' | null | 'unset' }): ProvidersResult`
    (RTL result plus `user`, `queryClient`, `router`; queries never retry); `src/test/msw/server.ts`: `server`. e2e:
    `test`, `expect` from `e2e/fixtures.ts` (viewer storage state by default).
- Consumes (Plan 06 response models as written; C8 path templates `/api/events/{date}` and `/api/shooters/{id}`, which
  Plan 06 binds with `Path(alias=…)`):
  - `GET /api/meta` → `MetaOut = { app_version: string; data_version: number; first_event_date: string | null; last_event_date: string | null; first_score_date: string | null; last_score_date: string | null; n_events: number; n_scored_events: number; n_held_events: number; n_station_events: number; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; last_rebuild_at: string | null }`.
  - `GET /api/events?year=&round_type=` → `EventSummaryOut[]`, `EventSummaryOut = { event_date: string; round_type: 'sporting' | 'super_sporting' | 'unknown'; round_type_source: string; head_count: number | null; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; has_scores: boolean; has_stations: boolean; results_complete: boolean; median: number | null; top_score: number | null; difficulty: number | null; condition: string | null; winners: { shooter_id: number; display_name: string; score: number }[] }`.
  - `GET /api/events/{date}` → `EventDetailOut = { event_date: string; round_type; round_type_source: string; head_count: number | null; has_scores: boolean; has_stations: boolean; results_complete: boolean; n_rounds: number; n_shooters: number; median: number | null; mean: number | null; stdev: number | null; top_score: number | null; difficulty: number | null; results: EventResultOut[]; weather: EventWeatherOut | null; stations: StationMatrixOut | null; notables: NotableOut[]; vs_prev: VsPrevOut | null }`,
    `EventResultOut = { round_id: number; shooter_id: number; display_name: string; name_key: string; shooter_status: string; ordinal: number; score: number; gauge_class: string | null; is_best_round: boolean; event_rank: number | null; percentile: number | null; adjusted: number | null; expected: number | null; residual: number | null; mu_before: number | null; mu_after: number | null; rating_delta: number | null }`
    (this task reads `display_name`, `is_best_round`, `event_rank` and the event totals).
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}?round_type=` (404 `shooter_not_found` for an unknown or merged-away id) →
    `ShooterDetailOut = { shooter_id: number; display_name: string; status: string; deceased: boolean; first_event: string; last_event: string; left_censored: boolean; current_mu: number | null; current_var: number | null; stats: { n_rounds: number; n_events: number; avg_score: number | null; median_score: number | null; best_score: number | null; avg_adjusted: number | null; wins: number; podiums: number; avg_percentile: number | null }; odometer: { clays_thrown: number; clays_broken: number; hit_pct: number | null; rounds: number; events: number; years_active: number; current_streak: number; longest_streak: number; favorite_month: number | null; trophies: number }; pbs: { scope: 'overall' | 'year'; key: string; score: number; event_date: string; round_id: number }[] }`.
  - `GET /api/shooters/{id}/rounds?round_type=` → `ShooterRoundOut[]`, `ShooterRoundOut = { round_id: number; event_date: string; ordinal: number; score: number; gauge_class: string | null; status: string | null; round_type: 'sporting' | 'super_sporting' | 'unknown'; is_best_round: boolean; event_rank: number | null; percentile: number | null; field_median: number | null; adjusted: number | null; expected: number | null; residual: number | null; mu_before: number | null; mu_after: number | null; condition: string | null }`.
- MSW rule: `home/mocks.ts` owns only the `*/api/meta` default handler — branch-free (a fixed response, because
  `mocks.ts` counts toward coverage) and exercised by `routes.test.tsx`. `/api/events*` belongs to `features/events` and
  `/api/shooters*` to `features/shooters` (Tasks 1 and 2, which run concurrently with this task), so every test here
  that reads them pins them with `server.use(...)`, `routes.test.tsx` included.
- Produces:
  - `features/home/widgets.ts`: `export type HomeWidget = { id: string; order: number; slot: 'main' | 'me'; Component: FC<{ meId: number | null }> }`;
    `collectHomeWidgets(modules: Record<string, { homeWidget?: HomeWidget }>): HomeWidget[]` (sorted by `order`, then
    `id`); `widgetsForSlot(widgets: HomeWidget[], slot: HomeWidget['slot']): HomeWidget[]`;
    `export const homeWidgets: HomeWidget[]` (glob `../*/homeWidget.tsx`, eager). Plan 10 T7 (slot `me`) and Plan 11
    T1/T4 export `homeWidget: HomeWidget` from `src/features/<name>/homeWidget.tsx`. Slot rule: `main` widgets always
    render in the main column with `meId` (possibly `null`); `me` widgets render inside the me panel, below the
    odometer, only when a me id is set and its profile has loaded. Widgets render themselves (no wrapper card).
  - `HomePage({ widgets?: HomeWidget[] })` (default: the glob result, so page tests pin `widgets` and later plugins never
    change a Plan 08 test). The me panel's last result is the best round on the shooter's latest attended date: score,
    finish (the Quigley ordinal of `event_rank`, e.g. `16th`; `—` when null) and rating move (`mu_after − mu_before`).
  - Route `/` (index); nav `{ label: 'Home', path: '/', icon: House, order: 10, mobileTab: true }`.
  - `features/home/mocks.ts`: the default `GET /api/meta` handler.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the e2e spec against a fresh stack to verify it fails**

Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 run concurrently in separate worktrees and every stack publishes host port 8080, so this task
builds its own image tag, runs Compose as its own project and holds the machine-wide lock directory
`/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime. The
`EXIT` trap tears the stack down and releases the lock even though this run fails. A lock with no
`docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is left over from a crashed run: `rmdir` it. Run from the
worktree root (`rm -rf secrets` deletes only this worktree's generated, gitignored dev secrets so the known e2e
passwords are used):
```bash
ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" && cd "$ROOT"
TAG=08-4
dc() { docker compose -p "sc-$TAG" -f "$ROOT/compose.yaml" -f "$ROOT/compose.test.yaml" "$@"; }
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-backend:$TAG" backend
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-frontend:$TAG" -f frontend/Dockerfile .
rm -rf secrets && VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin scripts/dev-secrets.sh
(cd frontend && pnpm exec playwright install chromium)
until mkdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock 2>/dev/null; do sleep 5; done
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `format.ts`, `widgets.ts` and `WidgetSlot`**

Create `frontend/src/features/home/format.ts`:
```ts
const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });

/** '2026-09-27' → 'Sep 27, 2026' (UTC in, UTC out: the day never shifts). */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

export function round1(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
}

/** 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th … 11th–13th, 21st … */
export function ordinal(n: number): string {
  const mod100 = n % 100;
  if (mod100 >= 11 && mod100 <= 13) return `${n}th`;
  switch (n % 10) {
    case 1:
      return `${n}st`;
    case 2:
      return `${n}nd`;
    case 3:
      return `${n}rd`;
    default:
      return `${n}th`;
  }
}

/** +0.8 / −0.3 (U+2212) / 0.0; rounds before choosing the sign; null → '—'. */
export function formatSigned(value: number | null, digits = 1): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  const rounded = Number(value.toFixed(digits));
  if (rounded === 0) return (0).toFixed(digits);
  const magnitude = Math.abs(rounded).toFixed(digits);
  return rounded > 0 ? `+${magnitude}` : `−${magnitude}`;
}

export function formatScore(value: number | null): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/home/widgets.ts`:
```ts
import type { FC } from 'react';

/**
 * Extension point (C10): a feature exports `homeWidget` from `src/features/<name>/homeWidget.tsx`. `main` widgets render
 * in the main column with `meId` (possibly null); `me` widgets render inside the me panel once a me profile has loaded.
 */
export type HomeWidget = {
  id: string;
  order: number;
  slot: 'main' | 'me';
  Component: FC<{ meId: number | null }>;
};

type WidgetModule = { homeWidget?: HomeWidget };

export function collectHomeWidgets(modules: Record<string, WidgetModule>): HomeWidget[] {
  return Object.values(modules)
    .flatMap((m) => (m.homeWidget ? [m.homeWidget] : []))
    .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
}

export function widgetsForSlot(widgets: HomeWidget[], slot: HomeWidget['slot']): HomeWidget[] {
  return widgets.filter((w) => w.slot === slot);
}

export const homeWidgets: HomeWidget[] = collectHomeWidgets(
  import.meta.glob<WidgetModule>('../*/homeWidget.tsx', { eager: true }),
);
```

Create `frontend/src/features/home/components/WidgetSlot.tsx`:
```tsx
import { widgetsForSlot } from '../widgets';
import type { HomeWidget } from '../widgets';

export function WidgetSlot({ slot, widgets, meId }: { slot: HomeWidget['slot']; widgets: HomeWidget[]; meId: number | null }) {
  return (
    <>
      {widgetsForSlot(widgets, slot).map(({ id, Component }) => (
        <Component key={id} meId={meId} />
      ))}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  it('shows a load failure', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/meta', () => HttpResponse.json(homeMeta)),
      http.get('*/api/events', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'Internal server error' } }, { status: 500 }),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<ClubPulse />);
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Add the meta, event and season hooks and implement `LatestEventCard` and `ClubPulse`**

Add these imports at the top of `frontend/src/features/home/api.ts`, above `import type { paths }`, and append the three
hooks (the Step 8 tests are their first failing tests). Their query keys equal the ones `features/events` uses for
the same requests, so the TanStack cache is shared at runtime:
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';
```
```ts
export function useMeta() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['/api/meta'], queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/meta')) });
}

/** `date === null` waits (no event is known yet). */
export function useEventDetail(date: string | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/events/{date}', date],
    // Only runs while enabled, i.e. with a non-null date.
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/events/{date}', { params: { path: { date: String(date) } } })),
    enabled: date !== null,
  });
}

/** `year === null` waits; appends the global round-type filter (C10). */
export function useSeasonEvents(year: number | null) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/events', { year, round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/events', { params: { query: { year: Number(year), round_type: roundTypes } } })),
    enabled: year !== null,
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/home/components/LatestEventCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import { useEventDetail, useMeta } from '../api';
import type { EventDetail } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatScore } from '../format';

export function winners(results: EventDetail['results']): string[] {
  return results
    .filter((r) => r.is_best_round && r.event_rank === 1)
    .map((r) => r.display_name)
    .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

export function attendanceText(headCount: number | null): string {
  return headCount === null ? 'No scores recorded' : `Attendance only — ${headCount} shooters, no scores recorded`;
}

function LatestEventBody({ event }: { event: EventDetail }) {
  const top = winners(event.results);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Link to={`/events/${event.event_date}`} className="text-lg font-medium">
        {formatDay(event.event_date)}
      </Link>
      {event.has_scores ? (
        <>
          <p>{`${top.length > 1 ? 'Winners' : 'Winner'}: ${top.join(' & ')} — ${formatScore(event.top_score)}`}</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <Stat label="Shooters" value={String(event.n_shooters)} />
            <Stat label="Median" value={formatScore(event.median)} />
            <Stat label="Top score" value={formatScore(event.top_score)} />
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <p>{attendanceText(event.head_count)}</p>
      )}
      <Link to={`/events/${event.event_date}`} className="min-h-11 self-start underline">
        Full results
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

export function LatestEventCard() {
  const meta = useMeta();
  const date = meta.data?.last_event_date ?? null;
  const event = useEventDetail(date);

  function body(): ReactNode {
    if (meta.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-32" />;
    if (meta.isError) return <EmptyState title="Couldn't load the latest event" description={meta.error.message} />;
    if (date === null) return <EmptyState title="No events yet" description="An admin can upload the scores workbook." />;
    if (event.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-32" />;
    if (event.isError) return <EmptyState title="Couldn't load the latest event" description={event.error.message} />;
    return <LatestEventBody event={event.data} />;
  }

  return <Card title="Latest event">{body()}</Card>;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/home/components/ClubPulse.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { barOption } from '../../../components/charts/builders/bar';
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import type { TabularData } from '../../../components/charts/types';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { Stat } from '../../../components/ui/Stat';
import { useMeta, useSeasonEvents } from '../api';
import type { EventSummary } from '../api';
import { round1 } from '../format';

const turnout = (e: EventSummary) => e.head_count ?? e.n_shooters;

export function pulseStats(events: EventSummary[]) {
  const scored = events.filter((e) => e.has_scores);
  const tops = scored.flatMap((e) => (e.top_score === null ? [] : [e.top_score]));
  return {
    held: scored.length,
    avgTurnout: scored.length === 0 ? null : round1(scored.map(turnout).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scored.length),
    seasonHigh: tops.length === 0 ? null : Math.max(...tops),
  };
}

export function turnoutModel(events: EventSummary[]): TabularData & { option: EChartsOption } {
  const columns: TabularData['columns'] = [
    { key: 'event_date', label: 'Date', type: 'date' },
    { key: 'shooters', label: 'Shooters', type: 'int' },
  ];
  const rows = events
    .filter((e) => e.has_scores)
    .sort((a, b) => a.event_date.localeCompare(b.event_date))
    .map((e) => ({ event_date: e.event_date, shooters: turnout(e) }));
  return { columns, rows, option: barOption({ columns, rows }, { x: 'event_date', y: ['shooters'] }) };
}

export function ClubPulse() {
  const meta = useMeta();
  const last = meta.data?.last_event_date ?? null;
  const year = last === null ? null : Number(last.slice(0, 4));
  const events = useSeasonEvents(year);

  function body(): ReactNode {
    if (meta.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-40" />;
    if (year === null) return <EmptyState title="No season yet" />;
    if (events.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-40" />;
    if (events.isError) return <EmptyState title="Couldn't load this season" description={events.error.message} />;
    const stats = pulseStats(events.data);
    if (stats.held === 0) return <EmptyState title="No scored events this season yet" />;
    const model = turnoutModel(events.data);
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">{`${year} season`}</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <Stat label="Events held" value={String(stats.held)} />
          <Stat label="Avg turnout" value={String(stats.avgTurnout)} />
          <Stat label="Season high" value={String(stats.seasonHigh)} />
        </div>
        <ChartFrame title="Turnout this season" option={model.option} columns={model.columns} rows={model.rows}
          csvName={`turnout-${year}`} ariaLabel={`Turnout per event in ${year}`} urlKey="pulse" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <Card title="Club pulse">{body()}</Card>;
}
```
(When `meta` fails, `year` stays `null`, so the card says "No season yet" — the latest-event card beside it shows the
meta error.)

```ts
/** Detail + odometer for the remembered "me"; 404 when the id was merged away (Review Focus #2). */
export function useShooterDetail(id: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}', id, { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}', { params: { path: { id }, query: { round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}

export function useShooterRounds(id: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}/rounds', id, { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}/rounds', { params: { path: { id }, query: { round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/home/components/MePanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/errors';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../components/ui/Skeleton';
import { clearMe } from '../../../lib/me';
import { useShooterDetail, useShooterRounds } from '../api';
import type { ShooterDetail, ShooterRound } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatSigned, ordinal, round1 } from '../format';
import type { HomeWidget } from '../widgets';
import { WidgetSlot } from './WidgetSlot';

export type LastResult = { date: string; score: number; rank: number | null; ratingMove: number | null };

/** The best round on the latest attended date (the top score when metrics are not computed yet). */
export function lastResult(rounds: ShooterRound[]): LastResult | null {
  if (rounds.length === 0) return null;
  const date = rounds.reduce((latest, r) => (r.event_date > latest ? r.event_date : latest), '');
  const day = rounds.filter((r) => r.event_date === date);
  const best = day.find((r) => r.is_best_round) ?? day.reduce((a, b) => (b.score > a.score ? b : a));
  const ratingMove = best.mu_before === null || best.mu_after === null ? null : round1(best.mu_after - best.mu_before);
  return { date, score: best.score, rank: best.event_rank, ratingMove };
}

function LastResultView({ result }: { result: LastResult }) {
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
      <div>
        <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">Last out</dt>
        <dd>
          <Link to={`/events/${result.date}`}>{formatDay(result.date)}</Link>
        </dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">Score · finish</dt>
        <dd className="tabular-nums">{`${result.score} · ${result.rank === null ? '—' : ordinal(result.rank)}`}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">Rating move</dt>
        <dd className="tabular-nums">{formatSigned(result.ratingMove)}</dd>
      </div>
    </dl>
  );
}

function MeOdometer({ odometer }: { odometer: ShooterDetail['odometer'] }) {
  const items = [
    ['Clays broken', odometer.clays_broken],
    ['Events', odometer.events],
    ['Current streak', odometer.current_streak],
  ] as const;
  return (
    <ul aria-label="Your odometer" className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
      {items.map(([label, value]) => (
        <li key={label} className="flex flex-col">
          <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">{label}</span>
          <span className="tabular-nums">{value.toLocaleString('en-US')}</span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

function MeDetails({ meId, onCleared, widgets }: { meId: number; onCleared: () => void; widgets: HomeWidget[] }) {
  const detail = useShooterDetail(meId);
  const rounds = useShooterRounds(meId);

  if (detail.isPending) return <Skeleton className="h-40" />;
  if (detail.isError) {
    if (detail.error instanceof ApiError && detail.error.status === 404) {
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <p>We couldn’t find your shooter profile — it may have been merged into another name.</p>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              clearMe();
              onCleared();
            }}
          >
            Choose again
          </Button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return <EmptyState title="Couldn't load your panel" description={detail.error.message} />;
  }

  let result: ReactNode;
  if (rounds.isPending) result = <Skeleton className="h-12" />;
  else if (rounds.isError) result = <p className="text-text-muted">Last result unavailable.</p>;
  else {
    const last = lastResult(rounds.data);
    result = last === null ? <p className="text-text-muted">No rounds yet.</p> : <LastResultView result={last} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">
        <Link to={`/shooters/${detail.data.shooter_id}`}>{detail.data.display_name}</Link>
      </h2>
      {result}
      <MeOdometer odometer={detail.data.odometer} />
      <WidgetSlot slot="me" widgets={widgets} meId={meId} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function MePanel({ meId, onCleared, widgets }: { meId: number | null; onCleared: () => void; widgets: HomeWidget[] }) {
  return (
    <Card title="Your panel">
      {meId === null ? (
        <p>
          Find yourself in <Link to="/shooters">Shooters</Link> and tap “That’s me” to see your last result, rating move
          and odometer here.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <MeDetails meId={meId} onCleared={onCleared} widgets={widgets} />
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { House } from 'lucide-react';
import type { RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import type { NavItem } from '../../app/registry';

/** Home (Plan 08 T4): latest event, club pulse, the "me" panel and the homeWidget glob host. */
export const routes: RouteObject[] = [
  {
    index: true,
    lazy: async () => ({ Component: (await import('./pages/HomePage')).HomePage }),

];
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 07, verbatim from Plan 07's code):
  - `src/api/client.ts`: `api` (openapi-fetch `createClient<paths>`) and
    `unwrap<T>(request: Promise<FetchResult<T>>): Promise<T>` (any non-2xx throws `ApiError`);
    `src/api/errors.ts`: `class ApiError extends Error { readonly status: number; readonly code: string }` whose
    `message` is the server's `error.message`; `paths` from the generated `src/api/schema.d.ts` (`import type`).
  - `src/app/registry.ts`: `interface NavItem { label: string; path: string; icon: LucideIcon; order: number; mobileTab?: boolean; adminOnly?: boolean }`.
  - `src/components/ui/`: `Card({ title?: ReactNode; subtitle?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; className?: string; children?: ReactNode })`
    (role `region` named by its title); `EmptyState({ title: string; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode })`;
    `Skeleton({ label?: string; lines?: number; className?: string })`;
    `Button(ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: 'primary' | 'tonal' | 'ghost' | 'danger'; loading?: boolean; icon?: ReactNode })`
    (default `variant="primary"`, default `type="button"`; there is no `secondary` variant — secondary actions use
    `tonal`).
  - `src/test/render.tsx`: `renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, options?: { route?: string; path?: string; queryClient?: QueryClient; role?: 'viewer' | 'admin' | null | 'unset' }): ProvidersResult`
    (`ui` mounted at `path`, default `'*'`; RTL result plus `user`, `queryClient`, `router`; queries never retry) and
    `renderRoutes(routes: RouteObject[], options?: RenderOptions): ProvidersResult` (same options; a route tree in a
    memory router inside the production providers); `src/test/msw/server.ts`: `server`.
  - `src/features/auth/components/RequireRole.tsx` (Plan 07 T3):
    `RequireRole({ role = 'viewer', children }: { role?: 'viewer' | 'admin'; children: ReactNode })` — a viewer under
    `role="admin"` gets `EmptyState` titled "Admins only"; Plan 07 D10 has admin pages wrap their elements in it
    (Decision D13).
  - e2e: `test`, `expect` from `e2e/fixtures.ts`; Plan 04 T1's `e2e/authState.ts` exports `VIEWER_STATE`
    (`'e2e/.auth/viewer.json'`) and `ADMIN_STATE` (`'e2e/.auth/admin.json'`), which the `setup` project (Plan 04 T1/T2)
    writes after seeding both fixture workbooks; admin specs use `test.use({ storageState: ADMIN_STATE })` (Plan 04 D19),
    and `playwright.config.ts` already imports `VIEWER_STATE` from `./e2e/authState` (Plan 04 T1).
- Consumes (Plan 04 T2 routes, C5/C8 models; admin path parameters keep C8's `{id}` via `Path(alias="id")`):
  - `GET /api/admin/imports` → `ImportSummary[]`, `ImportSummary = { id: number; kind: 'scores' | 'stations'; filename: string; status: 'pending' | 'committed' | 'discarded' | 'rolled_back'; uploaded_at: string; committed_at: string | null; rolled_back_at: string | null; sha256: string }`.
  - `POST /api/admin/imports` (multipart field `file`) → `ImportPreview = { import_id: number; kind: 'scores' | 'stations'; filename: string; duplicate_of: number | null; findings: FindingOut[]; diff: ScoresDiff | StationsDiff; requires_removal_confirmation: boolean }`;
    400 `unreadable_file`, 413 `payload_too_large`. A re-upload of a file whose import is `pending` or `committed`
    stages nothing and returns that import's stored preview with `import_id === duplicate_of` (and its original
    filename) — Plan 03 Decision 7.
  - `GET /api/admin/imports/{id}` → `ImportPreview` (404 for an unknown id; `duplicate_of` is always `null` here).
    `ImportPreview` has no status: the status comes from `GET /api/admin/imports`.
  - `POST /api/admin/imports/{id}/commit` body `{ confirm_removals: boolean }` → `{ job_id: number }`; 409
    `removals_not_confirmed` / `not_pending`.
  - `POST /api/admin/imports/{id}/discard` → 204; `POST /api/admin/imports/{id}/rollback` → `{ job_id: number }`.
  - `GET /api/admin/jobs/{id}` → `JobOut = { id: number; kind: string; status: string ('queued' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed'); attempts: number; error: string | null; created_at: string; started_at: string | null; finished_at: string | null }`.
  - `FindingOut = { code: string; severity: 'info' | 'warning' | 'error'; message: string; sheet?: string | null; row?: number | null; event_date?: string | null; name?: string | null }`
    — Plan 03 T3's model gives the four location fields a `= None` default, and FastAPI/pydantic leave defaulted fields
    out of `required` in the response schema, so the generated type marks them optional (the server always sends them;
    code reads them with `?? null`);
    `ScoresDiff = { events_added: string[]; events_removed: string[]; rows_added: number; rows_removed: number; rows_changed: number; new_names: string[]; possible_duplicates: [string, string][]; attendance_changed: number }`;
    `StationsDiff = { events_added: string[]; events_replaced: string[]; events_unchanged: string[]; sheets_skipped: string[] }`.
  - `GET /api/shooters?q=` (viewer, Plan 06 T6) for `ShooterPicker` → `ShooterSummaryOut[]`, `ShooterSummaryOut = { shooter_id: number; display_name: string; status: string; first_event: string; last_event: string; n_rounds: number; n_events: number; active: boolean; mu: number | null }`
    (server-side case-insensitive name search).
- MSW rule: `admin/mocks.ts` owns the `*/api/admin/imports*` and `*/api/admin/jobs/*` default handlers. They are
  branch-free (a fixed response, at most parameterised by a path param), because `mocks.ts` counts toward coverage, and
  `routes.test.tsx` runs on them on purpose so every handler line executes; every other test pins what it reads with
  `server.use(...)`. `/api/shooters` belongs to `features/shooters`, so every test that searches pins it.
- Rules this task implements:
  - Preview (Decision D14): a duplicate (`duplicate_of !== null`, returned by Plan 03 with `import_id === duplicate_of`,
    i.e. the existing import) shows "Already imported as import #N. Nothing to commit." and no actions, unless that
    import is still `pending`: then it shows "Already uploaded as import #N, which is still pending: review it below,
    then commit or discard it." and keeps Commit and Discard. Discard is offered for every `pending` import. Commit is
    disabled while removals are unconfirmed,
    while the status is not `pending`, and after a successful commit; ERROR findings never disable it. Findings are
    shown in three sections: "Will not be imported" (severity `error`), "Station vs score" (`station_score_mismatch`,
    `station_name_unmatched`, `station_round_ambiguous` that are not errors) and "Other findings" (grouped by code,
    warnings before info).
  - Jobs: `useJob(id)` polls `GET /api/admin/jobs/{id}` every 1000 ms until `done` or `failed`; `JobProgress` calls
    `onDone` once per job id. Rollback needs a second inline click ("Confirm roll back"); no `window.confirm`.
  - Routing (Decision D13): both routes wrap their page in `<RequireRole role="admin">` (Plan 07 D10); any admin call
    that still returns 403 renders the admins-only message through `AdminError`.
- Produces (consumed by Tasks 7 and 8):
  - `features/admin/api.ts`: `export type JsonOf<Op>`; types `ImportSummary`, `ImportPreview`, `Finding`, `ScoresDiff`,
    `StationsDiff`, `Job`, `ShooterMatch`; `type ShooterOption = { shooter_id: number; display_name: string }`;
    `JOB_POLL_MS = 1000`; `useJob(id: number)`; `useShooterSearch(q: string)` (waits for 2+ characters; query key
    `['/api/shooters', { q, active: false }]`).
  - `features/admin/format.ts`: `formatTimestamp(iso: string | null): string` (club time, `—` for null),
    `formatDay(iso: string): string`, `dateList(dates: string[]): string`, `statusLabel(status: string): string`,
    `kindLabel(kind: string): string`.
  - `features/admin/mocks.ts`: `doneJob: Job`, `shooterMatches: ShooterMatch[]` (Hadley, Ike #3 with 267 rounds;
    Crimson, Al #90 with 1 round) and the default `handlers`.
  - `features/admin/components/JobProgress.tsx`: `JobProgress({ jobId: number; label: string; onDone?: () => void })`
    — renders `role="status"` text `"<label>: Queued…|Running…|Done|Failed: <error>"` (or
    `"<label>: couldn't check job #<id>"`), calls `onDone` once per job.
  - `features/admin/components/ShooterPicker.tsx`: `ShooterPicker({ label: string; value: ShooterOption | null; onChange: (value: ShooterOption | null) => void })`
    — a searchbox named `label`; matches are buttons `"<display_name> · <n> rounds"`; a chosen shooter shows a
    `Change` button.
  - `features/admin/components/AdminError.tsx`: `AdminError({ error: Error })` (`role="alert"`; 403 → "Admins only —
    sign in with the admin password.", otherwise `error.message`) and `adminErrorMessage(error: Error): string`.
  - Routes `/admin`, `/admin/imports/:id`; nav `{ label: 'Imports', path: '/admin', icon: Upload, order: 900, adminOnly: true }`.
  - Playwright project `admin-mutations` (desktop only, one worker, after every read-only spec).

Create `frontend/e2e/admin-mutations.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { ADMIN_STATE } from './authState';
import { expect, test } from './fixtures';

// Project `admin-mutations` (Step 2): desktop only, one worker, after every read-only spec.
// Needs a fresh stack: imports are immutable, so a second run sees the variant as a duplicate.
test.use({ storageState: ADMIN_STATE });

test('re-uploading a seeded workbook shows the already-imported notice', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/admin');
  await page.getByLabel('Workbook (.xlsx)').setInputFiles('../backend/tests/fixtures/stations_2026-09-27.xlsx');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(/^Already imported as import #\d+\. Nothing to commit\.$/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' })).toHaveCount(0);
});

test('upload, commit and roll back a stations variant', async ({ page }) => {
  test.setTimeout(300_000);
  await page.goto('/admin');
  await page.getByLabel('Workbook (.xlsx)').setInputFiles('e2e/fixtures/stations_variant.xlsx');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Station vs score' })).toContainText('Marsden, Wylie');
  await expect(page.getByText('Weeks replaced')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Rebuilding live data: Done')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 120_000 });

  await page.goto('/admin');
  const row = page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Import history' }).getByRole('row', { name: /stations_variant\.xlsx/ });
  await expect(row).toContainText('Committed');
  await row.getByRole('button', { name: 'Roll back' }).click();
  await row.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm roll back' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(/^Rolling back #\d+: Done$/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 120_000 });
  await expect(row).toContainText('Rolled back');
});
```

The mutation fixture is the stations fixture loaded with `data_only=True`, tab `9 13 26` deleted and `9 6 26!B31`
(Marsden, Wylie, station 4) changed 4 → 5, so his station total (31) disagrees with his recorded score (30). Its preview
therefore shows exactly one "Station vs score" finding (Marsden, Wylie) and `2026-09-06` under replaced weeks. No earlier
plan creates `frontend/e2e/fixtures/`, so make it first. Run (worktree root):
```bash
mkdir -p frontend/e2e/fixtures
uv run --no-project --with openpyxl python - frontend/e2e/fixtures/stations_variant.xlsx <<'PY'
import sys
import warnings

from openpyxl import load_workbook

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore", message="Data Validation extension is not supported")
wb = load_workbook("backend/tests/fixtures/stations_2026-09-27.xlsx", data_only=True)
del wb["9 13 26"]
ws = wb["9 6 26"]
assert ws["A31"].value == "Marsden, Wylie" and ws["B31"].value == 4, (ws["A31"].value, ws["B31"].value)
ws["B31"] = 5  # station 4: 4 -> 5, so the station total is 31 vs the recorded score 30
wb.save(sys.argv[1])
PY
uv run --no-project --with openpyxl python -c "from openpyxl import load_workbook; wb = load_workbook('frontend/e2e/fixtures/stations_variant.xlsx', data_only=True); print(wb.sheetnames, [c.value for c in wb['9 6 26'][31]][:8])"
```
Expected output of the check: `['9 6 26', 'Name List (2)'] ['Marsden, Wylie', 5, 4, 3, 4, 6, 2, 7]`. The file is binary
test data; commit it as generated. Re-running `admin-mutations` needs a fresh stack (imports are immutable, so a second
upload of the variant is a duplicate); every e2e run below starts one.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the e2e specs against a fresh stack to verify they fail**

Tasks 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 run concurrently in separate worktrees and every stack publishes host port 8080, so this task
builds its own image tag, runs Compose as its own project and holds the machine-wide lock directory
`/tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock` (`mkdir` is atomic and portable; macOS has no `flock`) for exactly one stack lifetime. The
`EXIT` trap tears the stack down and releases the lock even though this run fails. A lock with no
`docker ps --filter publish=8080` container behind it is left over from a crashed run: `rmdir` it. Run from the
worktree root (`rm -rf secrets` deletes only this worktree's generated, gitignored dev secrets so the known e2e
passwords are used):
```bash
ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" && cd "$ROOT"
TAG=08-5a
dc() { docker compose -p "sc-$TAG" -f "$ROOT/compose.yaml" -f "$ROOT/compose.test.yaml" "$@"; }
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-backend:$TAG" backend
docker build -t "ghcr.io/gitgat/sunday-clays-frontend:$TAG" -f frontend/Dockerfile .
rm -rf secrets && VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin scripts/dev-secrets.sh
(cd frontend && pnpm exec playwright install chromium)
until mkdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock 2>/dev/null; do sleep 5; done
trap 'dc down -v; rmdir /tmp/sunday-clays-e2e.lock' EXIT
IMAGE_TAG="$TAG" dc up -d --wait
(cd frontend && E2E_VIEWER_PASSWORD=e2e-viewer E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD=e2e-admin pnpm exec playwright test e2e/admin.spec.ts e2e/admin-mutations.spec.ts)
```
Expected: FAIL — `admin.spec.ts` at both viewports cannot find the "Imports" heading (no `/admin` page yet), so
`admin-mutations` is skipped as a dependent of failed projects; then the trap removes the stack and the lock.

```ts
describe('admin formatting', () => {
  it('formats timestamps in club time', () => {
    expect(formatTimestamp('2026-09-27T18:05:00Z')).toMatch(/^Sep 27, 2026, 11:05\sAM$/);
    expect(formatTimestamp(null)).toBe('—');
  });

  it('formats event dates without timezone drift', () => {
    expect(formatDay('2026-09-06')).toBe('Sep 6, 2026');
  });

  it.each([
    ['pending', 'Pending'],
    ['committed', 'Committed'],
    ['discarded', 'Discarded'],
    ['rolled_back', 'Rolled back'],
    ['odd', 'odd'],
  ])('status %s → %s', (status, want) => {
    expect(statusLabel(status)).toBe(want);
  });

  it.each([
    ['scores', 'Scores workbook'],
    ['stations', 'Station workbook'],
    ['other', 'other'],
  ])('kind %s → %s', (kind, want) => {
    expect(kindLabel(kind)).toBe(want);
  });

  it('lists dates or a dash', () => {
    expect(dateList(['2026-09-06', '2026-09-13'])).toBe('Sep 6, 2026, Sep 13, 2026');
    expect(dateList([])).toBe('—');
  });
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/admin/format.ts`:
```ts
const STAMP = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  dateStyle: 'medium',
  timeStyle: 'short',
  timeZone: 'America/Los_Angeles',
});
const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  pending: 'Pending',
  committed: 'Committed',
  discarded: 'Discarded',
  rolled_back: 'Rolled back',
};
const KIND_LABELS: Record<string, string> = { scores: 'Scores workbook', stations: 'Station workbook' };

/** timestamptz (UTC) → club wall-clock time. */
export function formatTimestamp(iso: string | null): string {
  return iso === null ? '—' : STAMP.format(new Date(iso));
}

export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

export function dateList(dates: string[]): string {
  return dates.length === 0 ? '—' : dates.map(formatDay).join(', ');
}

export function statusLabel(status: string): string {
  return STATUS_LABELS[status] ?? status;
}

export function kindLabel(kind: string): string {
  return KIND_LABELS[kind] ?? kind;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/admin/components/AdminError.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/errors';

export function adminErrorMessage(error: Error): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 403) return 'Admins only — sign in with the admin password.';
  return error.message;
}

/** Server-rejected admin action (Review Focus #5): the message sits next to the control that failed. */
export function AdminError({ error }: { error: Error }) {
  return (
    <p role="alert" className="text-error">
      {adminErrorMessage(error)}
    </p>
  );
}
```

```ts
import type { paths } from '../../api/schema';

export type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } } ? R : never;

export type ImportSummary = JsonOf<paths['/api/admin/imports']['get']>[number];
export type ImportPreview = JsonOf<paths['/api/admin/imports/{id}']['get']>;
export type Finding = ImportPreview['findings'][number];
export type ScoresDiff = Extract<ImportPreview['diff'], { rows_added: number }>;
export type StationsDiff = Exclude<ImportPreview['diff'], ScoresDiff>;
export type Job = JsonOf<paths['/api/admin/jobs/{id}']['get']>;
export type ShooterMatch = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters']['get']>[number];
export type ShooterOption = { shooter_id: number; display_name: string };

export const JOB_POLL_MS = 1000;
```

Add these imports at the top of `frontend/src/features/admin/api.ts`, above `import type { paths }`, and append the two
hooks (the Step 10 tests are their first failing tests):
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
```
```ts
/** Polls every JOB_POLL_MS until the job is done or failed. */
export function useJob(id: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/admin/jobs/{id}', id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/admin/jobs/{id}', { params: { path: { id } } })),
    refetchInterval: (query) => {
      const status = query.state.data?.status;
      return status === 'done' || status === 'failed' ? false : JOB_POLL_MS;
    },
  });
}

/** Viewer shooter search for admin pickers; waits for 2+ characters. Key shape matches features/shooters. */
export function useShooterSearch(q: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters', { q, active: false }],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters', { params: { query: { q } } })),
    enabled: q.length >= 2,
  });
}
```
Create `frontend/src/features/admin/components/JobProgress.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useJob } from '../api';
import type { Job } from '../api';

export function jobStatusText(job: Pick<Job, 'status' | 'error'>): string {
  switch (job.status) {
    case 'queued':
      return 'Queued…';
    case 'running':
      return 'Running…';
    case 'done':
      return 'Done';
    case 'failed':
      return `Failed: ${job.error ?? 'unknown error'}`;
    default:
      return String(job.status);
  }
}

/** Live status of one worker job (polled every JOB_POLL_MS until done or failed); `onDone` fires once per job id. */
export function JobProgress({ jobId, label, onDone }: { jobId: number; label: string; onDone?: () => void }) {
  const job = useJob(jobId);
  const status = job.data?.status;
  const firedFor = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'done' && firedFor.current !== jobId) {
      firedFor.current = jobId;
      onDone?.();
    }
  }, [status, jobId, onDone]);

  let text: string;
  if (job.isError) text = `couldn't check job #${jobId}`;
  else if (job.data === undefined) text = 'Queued…';
  else text = jobStatusText(job.data);

  return (
    <p role="status" aria-live="polite" className="rounded-card border border-outline-variant p-3">
      {`${label}: ${text}`}
    </p>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/admin/components/ShooterPicker.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useDeferredValue, useId, useState } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { useShooterSearch } from '../api';
import type { ShooterOption } from '../api';
import { AdminError } from './AdminError';

export function ShooterPicker({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: ShooterOption | null;
  onChange: (value: ShooterOption | null) => void;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const term = useDeferredValue(q.trim());
  const results = useShooterSearch(term);

  if (value !== null) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">{`${label}:`}</span>
        <strong>{value.display_name}</strong>
        <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">{`#${value.shooter_id}`}</span>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onChange(null)}>
          Change
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  let matches: ReactNode = null;
  if (term.length >= 2) {
    if (results.isPending) matches = <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">Searching…</p>;
    else if (results.isError) matches = <AdminError error={results.error} />;
    else if (results.data.length === 0) matches = <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">No shooters match</p>;
    else
      matches = (
        <ul aria-label={`${label} matches`} className="flex flex-col">
          {results.data.slice(0, 8).map((s) => (
            <li key={s.shooter_id}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="min-h-11 w-full px-2 text-left"
                onClick={() => onChange({ shooter_id: s.shooter_id, display_name: s.display_name })}
              >
                {`${s.display_name} · ${s.n_rounds} rounds`}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-sm">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        type="search"
        value={q}
        onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        className="min-h-11 rounded-button border border-outline-variant bg-elevated px-3"
      />
      {matches}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
    expect(screen.getByText('Sep 6, 2026')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Skipped tabs: 9 20 26')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('flags removed rows even when no whole event disappears', () => {
    renderWithProviders(<DiffSummary diff={{ ...scoresPreview.diff, rows_removed: 3 }} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Removals' })).toHaveTextContent('missing 0 events and 3 rows');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('list', { name: 'Removed events' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../../test/msw/server';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../../test/render';
import { doneJob, scoresPreview, stalePreview } from '../mocks';
import { CommitPanel } from './CommitPanel';

function captureCommits() {
  const bodies: unknown[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.post('*/api/admin/imports/:id/commit', async ({ request }) => {
      bodies.push(await request.json());
      return HttpResponse.json({ job_id: 41 });
    }),
    http.get('*/api/admin/jobs/:id', () => HttpResponse.json(doneJob)),
  );
  return bodies;
}

describe('CommitPanel', () => {
  it('commit stays disabled until removals are confirmed', async () => {
    const bodies = captureCommits();
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderWithProviders(<CommitPanel preview={stalePreview} onCommitted={vi.fn()} />);
    const commit = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' });
    expect(commit).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: /removes the events and rows listed above/ }));
    expect(commit).toBeEnabled();
    await user.click(commit);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Rebuilding live data: Done')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(bodies).toEqual([{ confirm_removals: true }]);
  });

  it('commits without a checkbox when nothing is removed and reports when the rebuild is done', async () => {
    const bodies = captureCommits();
    const onCommitted = vi.fn();
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderWithProviders(<CommitPanel preview={scoresPreview} onCommitted={onCommitted} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('checkbox')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Rebuilding live data: Done')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(bodies).toEqual([{ confirm_removals: false }]);
    expect(onCommitted).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('commit conflict shows the server message', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.post('*/api/admin/imports/:id/commit', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'not_pending', message: 'Import 4 is not pending' } }, { status: 409 }),
      ),
    );
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderWithProviders(<CommitPanel preview={scoresPreview} onCommitted={vi.fn()} />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Import 4 is not pending');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' })).toBeEnabled();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useId } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { ImportPreview, ScoresDiff, StationsDiff } from '../api';
import { dateList, formatDay } from '../format';

function Row({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <dt className="text-xs text-text-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd>{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

function Removals({ diff }: { diff: ScoresDiff }) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-card border-2 border-error-container p-3">
      <h3 id={id} className="font-medium text-error">Removals</h3>
      <p>
        {`This file is missing ${diff.events_removed.length} events and ${diff.rows_removed} rows that are live now. Committing it removes them.`}
      </p>
      {diff.events_removed.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label="Removed events" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {diff.events_removed.map((d) => (
            <li key={d} className="rounded-button border border-error-container px-2">
              {formatDay(d)}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function ScoresDiffView({ diff }: { diff: ScoresDiff }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {(diff.events_removed.length > 0 || diff.rows_removed > 0) && <Removals diff={diff} />}
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Row label="Events added" value={dateList(diff.events_added)} />
        <Row label="Rows added" value={String(diff.rows_added)} />
        <Row label="Rows changed" value={String(diff.rows_changed)} />
        <Row label="Rows removed" value={String(diff.rows_removed)} />
        <Row label="Head counts changed" value={String(diff.attendance_changed)} />
      </dl>
      {diff.new_names.length > 0 && (
        <details>
          <summary>{`New names (${diff.new_names.length})`}</summary>
          <ul className="flex flex-col">
            {diff.new_names.map((n) => (
              <li key={n}>{n}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </details>
      )}
      {diff.possible_duplicates.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h3 className="font-medium">Possible duplicates</h3>
          <ul className="flex flex-col">
            {diff.possible_duplicates.map(([a, b]) => (
              <li key={`${a}|${b}`}>{`${a} ↔ ${b}`}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">New names are new shooters; merge real duplicates later under Identity.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function StationsDiffView({ diff }: { diff: StationsDiff }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Row label="Weeks added" value={dateList(diff.events_added)} />
        <Row label="Weeks replaced" value={dateList(diff.events_replaced)} />
        <Row label="Weeks unchanged" value={dateList(diff.events_unchanged)} />
      </dl>
      {diff.sheets_skipped.length > 0 && (
        <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">{`Skipped tabs: ${diff.sheets_skipped.join(', ')}`}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function DiffSummary({ diff }: { diff: ImportPreview['diff'] }) {
  return <Card title="Changes">{'rows_added' in diff ? <ScoresDiffView diff={diff} /> : <StationsDiffView diff={diff} />}</Card>;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/admin/components/FindingsList.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../../components/ui/Card';
import type { Finding } from '../api';
import { formatDay } from '../format';

const STATION_CODES = new Set(['station_score_mismatch', 'station_name_unmatched', 'station_round_ambiguous']);

export type FindingGroup = { code: string; severity: string; items: Finding[] };

/** Errors are excluded rows; non-error station codes get their own heading; the rest group by code, warnings first. */
export function groupFindings(findings: Finding[]): { excluded: Finding[]; station: Finding[]; other: FindingGroup[] } {
  const excluded = findings.filter((f) => f.severity === 'error');
  const station = findings.filter((f) => f.severity !== 'error' && STATION_CODES.has(f.code));
  const byCode = new Map<string, Finding[]>();
  for (const f of findings) {
    if (f.severity === 'error' || STATION_CODES.has(f.code)) continue;
    byCode.set(f.code, [...(byCode.get(f.code) ?? []), f]);
  }
  const rank = (severity: string) => (severity === 'warning' ? 0 : 1);
  const other = [...byCode.entries()]
    .map(([code, items]) => ({ code, severity: (items[0] as Finding).severity, items }))
    .sort((a, b) => rank(a.severity) - rank(b.severity) || a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
  return { excluded, station, other };
}

/** FindingOut's location fields default to None (Plan 03), so the generated type has them optional: read with `?? null`. */
export function findingLocation(f: Finding): string {
  const row = f.row ?? null;
  const eventDate = f.event_date ?? null;
  return [f.sheet ?? null, row === null ? null : `row ${row}`, eventDate === null ? null : formatDay(eventDate), f.name ?? null]
    .filter((part): part is string => part !== null)
    .join(' · ');
}

function FindingItem({ finding }: { finding: Finding }) {
  const location = findingLocation(finding);
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col">
      <span>{finding.message}</span>
      {location && <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">{location}</span>}
    </li>
  );
}

function FindingSection({ title, hint, items }: { title: string; hint: string; items: Finding[] }) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <h3 id={id} className="font-medium">{title}</h3>
      <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">{`${items.length} · ${hint}`}</p>
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {items.map((f, i) => (
          <FindingItem key={`${f.code}-${i}`} finding={f} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

export function FindingsList({ findings }: { findings: Finding[] }) {
  const { excluded, station, other } = groupFindings(findings);
  return (
    <Card title="Findings">
      {findings.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">No findings.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {excluded.length > 0 && (
            <FindingSection title="Will not be imported" hint="These rows or tabs are left out; the rest can still be committed." items={excluded} />
          )}
          {station.length > 0 && (
            <FindingSection title="Station vs score" hint="Station sheets that disagree with the scores; fix names with an alias under Data & ops." items={station} />
          )}
          {other.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <h3 className="font-medium">Other findings</h3>
              {other.map((g) => (
                <details key={g.code}>
                  <summary>{`${g.code} (${g.items.length}) · ${g.severity}`}</summary>
                  <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 pl-4">
                    {g.items.map((f, i) => (
                      <FindingItem key={`${g.code}-${i}`} finding={f} />
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </details>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/admin/components/CommitPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/Button';
import { useCommitImport } from '../api';
import type { ImportPreview } from '../api';
import { AdminError } from './AdminError';
import { JobProgress } from './JobProgress';

/** Review Focus #1: with removals, Commit stays disabled until the admin confirms them; ERROR findings never block. */
export function CommitPanel({ preview, onCommitted }: { preview: ImportPreview; onCommitted: () => void }) {
  const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false);
  const commit = useCommitImport();
  const checkboxId = useId();
  const needsConfirm = preview.requires_removal_confirmation;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {needsConfirm && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            id={checkboxId}
            type="checkbox"
            className="size-5"
            checked={confirmed}
            onChange={(e) => setConfirmed(e.target.checked)}
          />
          <label htmlFor={checkboxId}>I understand this upload removes the events and rows listed above</label>
        </div>
      )}
      <Button
        disabled={(needsConfirm && !confirmed) || commit.isPending || commit.isSuccess}
        onClick={() => commit.mutate({ id: preview.import_id, confirmRemovals: confirmed })}
      >
        Commit import
      </Button>
      {commit.isError && <AdminError error={commit.error} />}
      {commit.isSuccess && <JobProgress jobId={commit.data.job_id} label="Rebuilding live data" onDone={onCommitted} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';

/** Stands in for ImportDetailPage, whose status comes from the import list that a finished commit refreshes. */
function StatusHarness() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('pending');
  return (
    <>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setStatus('committed')}>
        List says committed
      </button>
      <ImportPreviewView preview={scoresPreview} status={status} />

  );
}

function renderPreview(preview: ImportPreview, status: string | undefined) {

    <Routes>
      <Route path="/admin/imports/:id" element={<ImportPreviewView preview={preview} status={status} />} />
      <Route path="/admin" element={<p>imports page</p>} />
    </Routes>,
    { route: `/admin/imports/${preview.import_id}` },
  );
}

describe('ImportPreviewView', () => {
  it('a pending import shows its changes, findings, commit and discard', () => {
    renderPreview(stalePreview, 'pending');

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard this upload' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

    expect(screen.getByText('Already imported as import #2. Nothing to commit.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Commit import' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Discard this upload' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('a re-upload of a still-pending file points at that import and keeps its actions', () => {
    renderPreview({ ...stalePreview, duplicate_of: stalePreview.import_id }, 'pending');

      screen.getByText('Already uploaded as import #3, which is still pending: review it below, then commit or discard it.'),

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Discard this upload' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../../test/msw/server';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../../test/render';
import { scoresPreview } from '../mocks';
import { UploadForm } from './UploadForm';

function PreviewProbe() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  return <p>{`preview ${String(id)}`}</p>;
}

function renderUpload() {
  return renderWithProviders(
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/admin" element={<UploadForm />} />
      <Route path="/admin/imports/:id" element={<PreviewProbe />} />
    </Routes>,
    { route: '/admin' },
  );
}

const workbook = () => new File(['PK'], 'scores_2026-10-04.xlsx', { type: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet' });

describe('UploadForm', () => {
  it('uploads the chosen workbook and opens its preview', async () => {
    let uploads = 0;
    server.use(
      http.post('*/api/admin/imports', () => {
        uploads += 1;
        return HttpResponse.json(scoresPreview);
      }),
    );
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderUpload();
    const submit = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' });
    expect(submit).toBeDisabled();
    await user.upload(screen.getByLabelText('Workbook (.xlsx)'), workbook());
    await user.click(submit);
    expect(await screen.findByText('preview 4')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(uploads).toBe(1);
  });

  it('upload of an unreadable file shows the server message', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.post('*/api/admin/imports', () =>
        HttpResponse.json(
          { error: { code: 'unreadable_file', message: 'This file could not be read as an Excel workbook' } },
          { status: 400 },
        ),
      ),
    );
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderUpload();
    await user.upload(screen.getByLabelText('Workbook (.xlsx)'), workbook());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('This file could not be read as an Excel workbook');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('clearing the chosen file disables upload, and a keyboard submit without a file sends nothing', async () => {
    let uploads = 0;
    server.use(
      http.post('*/api/admin/imports', () => {
        uploads += 1;
        return HttpResponse.json(scoresPreview);
      }),
    );
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderUpload();
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Workbook (.xlsx)');
    await user.upload(input, workbook());
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' })).toBeEnabled();
    await user.upload(input, []);
    const submit = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Upload and preview' });
    expect(submit).toBeDisabled();
    const form = submit.closest('form');
    if (form === null) throw new Error('upload form not found');
    fireEvent.submit(form);
    expect(uploads).toBe(0);
  });
});
```
